Dedication: 
  
to my mother inlaw juanita miller.  God used you 
to introduce me to the Word of God and ever 
since that day my life has never been the same.   
Thank you for your boldness and love and 
showing me Christianity with actions not just 
words.   Never give up and never give in God is 
seeing you through.  You have chose your future 
for the Glory of God.  Joshua 1:7-9.

ACKNOWLEDGMENTS 
 
First of all I would like to thank the Lord Jesus Christ for giving 
me wisdome,knowledge and a passion to get this very important 
Truth out, Glory gos to our God. 
 
I thank every one who supported in making this book may the Lord 
Bless you with a rich new annointing on your lifes, 1 corintians 
15:58 says what ever you do for the Lord will not be in vain.    
 
Thank you Pastor Mark (my Pastor)  for helping me edit this book 
and adding your own personal touch it is greatly appreciated.   
   
All the staff of Soldiers for God ministry get out there and pass this 
book out!  God Bless...

2 
 
FOREWORD 
 
I am happy to commend Anthony Lee’s book, Choosing Your 
Future, for the following reasons: 
 
It is biblical.  The author does not give easy answers to the 
problems arising from traditions because he believes they have 
spiritual causes and therefore need spiritual answers found only in 
the Scriptures.  Accordingly, he looks carefully into the biblical 
teaching relevant to the subject. 
 
It is practical.  Each chapter provides application.  The reader is 
encouraged to take practical steps to apply what he has learned to 
his experience.  This may take the form of a personal inventory, 
prayers for specific insight, or aids to developing spiritual 
disciplines and relationships which will assist in achieving victory 
over darkness. 
 
It is spiritual.  There is no shortage of books in the self-
improvement category and there are plenty of customers for such 
books.  At first glance this book may appear to be yet another, but 
this is not the case.  The author leads people to become what God 
created and redeemed them to be. 
 
It is pastoral.  Many case studies from the author’s personal 
ministry are used to establish his principles.  The reader can 
discern a fine blend of compassion and firmness.  The firmness has 
its roots in the authoritative Word of God and the compassion 
springs from the heart of one who has struggled and overcome. 
 
M. Darrell Rice 
Administrative Bishop 
Church of God/Great Lakes Region

4 
 
Choosing Your Future 
 
Introduction: 
My  name  is  Anthony  Lee,  and  I  am  the  Senior  Pastor  of  
“Soldiers  for  God  Ministries”  located  in  Chicago,  Illinois.    I  felt  
led  by  the  Holy  Spirit  to  write  this  Book,  because  there  are  many  
people  practicing  the  art  of  fortunetelling  who  do  not  understand  
what they are involved in; they believe it is just a scam or a way to 
make money.  I wrote this book not to expose fortune tellers to 
people, but to expose the truth to fortune tellers so they may be 
set free from the deception of the devil.  In this book, I will share 
what  the  Word  of  God  says  about  the  many  Sins  that  accompany  
the practice of Fortune telling. 
I  have  written  this  book,  because  I  frequently  come  into  
contact  with  people  who  do  not  understand  the  seriousness  of  the  
practice   of   Fortune   telling.      It   is   my   desire   to   help   people   
understand what the word of God says, and what the consequences
 
of  fortune  telling  are.    The  majority  of  this  Book  is  filled  with  
scripture  more  then  my  own  comments,  and  the  reason  I  have  
presented it in this way, is because I want people to realize that the 
words  in  this  book  are  not  my  personal  opinions,  but  what  the  
Bible has to say about the subject.   
I  am  no  stranger  to  the  art  of  fortunetelling,  for  I  myself  
have  practiced  it  in  my  life  prior  to  my  salvation.    I  am  not  
speaking out of ignorance, but from personal experience.   I myself 
4

Choosing Your Future 
 
Introduction: 
My  name  is  Anthony  Lee,  and  I  am  the  Senior  Pastor  of  
“Soldiers  for  God  Ministries”  located  in  Chicago,  Illinois.    I  felt  
led  by  the  Holy  Spirit  to  write  this  Book,  because  there  are  many  
people  practicing  the  art  of  fortunetelling  who  do  not  understand  
what they are involved in; they believe it is just a scam or a way to 
make money.  I wrote this book not to expose fortune tellers to 
people, but to expose the truth to fortune tellers so they may be 
set free from the deception of the devil.  In this book, I will share 
what  the  Word  of  God  says  about  the  many  Sins  that  accompany  
the practice of Fortune telling. 
I  have  written  this  book,  because  I  frequently  come  into  
contact  with  people  who  do  not  understand  the  seriousness  of  the  
practice   of   Fortune   telling.      It   is   my   desire   to   help   people   
understand what the word of God says, and what the consequences
 
of  fortune  telling  are.    The  majority  of  this  Book  is  filled  with  
scripture  more  then  my  own  comments,  and  the  reason  I  have  
presented it in this way, is because I want people to realize that the 
words  in  this  book  are  not  my  personal  opinions,  but  what  the  
Bible has to say about the subject.   
I  am  no  stranger  to  the  art  of  fortunetelling,  for  I  myself  
have  practiced  it  in  my  life  prior  to  my  salvation.    I  am  not  
speaking out of ignorance, but from personal experience.   I myself 

5 
 
have been delivered from this evil, and I want to help other people 
find their deliverance, also.  The Bible says “the truth shall set you 
free” and it is my prayer that as you receive this truth, you will be 
set  free  just  as  I  have  found  freedom  in  Jesus  Christ.    If  you  read  
this  book  with  a  desire  to  know  the  truth,  I  guarantee  you  will  be  
amazed and surprised in what God will reveal to you.   This book 
will  answer  many  of  your  questions  and  respond  to  your  excuses  
about fortune telling, including:  
• What   about   all   the   other   “scam”   businesses   people   are   
involved in?  
• Why is fortune telling so bad?
 
•  God knows my heart. 
•  I really help people.  
• It is not really witchcraft.   
All these questions will be answered and more. 
Many  pastors  I  have  talked  to  about  fortune  telling  say,  
“win  them  over  with  love,”  and  that  is  a  great  answer,  but  loving  
means  telling  the  truth.    If  you  seen  a  burning  building  and  you  
knew  people  were  inside,  would  you  lightly  tap  on  the  door  and  
whisper  “there’s  a  fire,”  or  would  you  try  to  bust  the  door  down  
and scream to the top of your lungs, “There is a Fire!”?  Of course, 
you  would  scream  out  to  save  the  people  inside?    It  is  our  
responsibility  to  inform  the  people  who  tell  fortune  the  truth,  so  
their souls can be saved them from the fires of hell!   
These are the reasons I wrote this book 
5 
 
have been delivered from this evil, and I want to help other people 
find their deliverance, also.  The Bible says “the truth shall set you 
free” and it is my prayer that as you receive this truth, you will be 
set  free  just  as  I  have  found  freedom  in  Jesus  Christ.    If  you  read

about fortune telling, including:  
• What   about   all   the   other   “scam”   businesses   people   are   
involved in?  
• Why is fortune telling so bad?
 
•  God knows my heart. 
•  I really help people.  
• It is not really witchcraft
.   
All these questions will be answered and more. 
Many  pastors  I  have  talked  to  about  fortune  telling  say,  
“win  them  over  with  love,”  and  that  is  a  great  answer,  but  loving  
means  telling  the  truth.    If  you  seen  a  burning  building  and  you  
knew  people  were  inside,  would  you  lightly  tap  on  the  door  and  
whisper  “there’s  a  fire,”  or  would  you  try  to  bust  the  door  down  
and scream to the top of your lungs, “There is a Fire!”?  Of course, 
you  would  scream  out  to  save  the  people  inside?    It  is  our  
responsibility  to  inform  the  people  who  tell  fortune  the  truth,  so  
their souls can be saved them from the fires of hell!   
These are the reasons I wrote this book 

6 
 
1.   As  a  Pastor  and  a  teacher,  the  Bible  tells  me  that  I  will  stand  in  
judgment  for  what  I  did  or  did  not  teach.    James  3:1    My  friends,  
not  many  of  you  should  become  teachers.  As  you  know,  we  
teachers will be judged with greater strictness than others.  
2. The  Bible  says  if  I  do  not  tell  the  people  the  knowledge  I  have  
received, their lives will be destroyed and God will reject me from 
being  a  Pastor  and  teacher.    Hosea  4:6    My  people  are  destroyed  
for  lack  of  knowledge;  because  you  have  rejected  knowledge,  I  
reject you from being a priest to me. And since you have forgotten 
the law of your God, I also will forget your children. 
3. The  Bible  also  tells  me  that  if  I  do  not  tell  people  the  truth,  their  
blood  will  be  on  my  hands.    Ezekiel  33:7-9    "Now,  mortal  man,  I  
am making you a lookout for the nation of Israel. You must pass on 
to them the warnings I give you.  If I announce that an evil person 
is going to die but you do not warn him to change his ways
 so that 
he can save his life, then he will die, still a sinner, and I will hold 
you responsible for his death.  If you do warn an evil person and 
he doesn't stop sinning, he will die, still a sinner, but your life will 
be spared."  
Hopefully, now you can see my convictions about this subject, 
and  why  I  feel  God  has  given  me  a  clear  mandate  to  write  this  
book.    I  have  heard  various  leaders  say  about  this  subject,  that  it  
would take a miracle for some people to give up their ways. That is 
absolutely  true;  it  will  take  nothing  less  then  the  miracle  of  the  
truth of Jesus Christ and the word of God...and it is our job to give

them  the  truth.        So  please  help  me  give  this  truth  to  the  people  
who so desperately need it. 
My wife, Gina, began telling fortune at the age of 10 years 
old and continued to practice the craft until she was 19.  Gina grew 
up in a family that practiced fortune telling, and I also grew up in a 
family  that  practiced  Fortune  telling.    When  Gina  and  I  were  
married,  we  continued  to  Practice  Fortune  telling.      Together,  we  
deceived many people to get what we desired from them.  We did 
this  for  about  a  year,  until  we  became  TRUE  Christians  and  were  
convicted  by  the  Holy  Spirit,  and  stopped  telling  fortune  along  
with many other sins that were in our lives. 
I  wrote  this  book  to  give  truth  to  the  fortuneteller.    Not  to  
condemn  people,  but  because  I  have  concern  and  love  for  people,  
and  I  desire  to  teach  what  God  has  taught  me.      In  2005,  my  
Mother Janette Lee died.  I was so depressed and worried, because 
I thought she went to hell for the sin that was in her life...including 
the  sin  of  fortunetelling.    However,  through  some  good  Christian  
brothers  and  sisters,  God  revealed  to  me  that  she  had  repented  
before she died.  I am so glad that  she  got  a  chance  to  repent,  but  
we  are  not  all  given  that  chance.    The  Bible  says  that  we  are  not  
promised  tomorrow.  ..You  never  know  what  can  happen  at  any  
moment.  The truth needs to be revealed before it is too late! 
So please take time to read this book so you will know the 
truth.  Many people say, “What I don’t know wont hurt me,” well 
that  is  not  true.    The  Bible  says  in  Acts  17:30,  “there  once  was  a  
time  when  God  winked  at  ignorance,  but  no  more.”    Please  read

Chapter 1: 
Becoming Their God. 
 
The Bible says in Exodus 20:2-3  “I am Jehovah thy God, who 
brought thee out of the land of Egypt, out of the house of bondage.  
Thou  shalt  have  no  other  gods  before  me.”  When  you  start  
telling  fortune  to  people,  they  begin  calling  and  placing  their  
trust in you, but they are supposed to call and place their trust 
in God.  We should take advice from God first and foremost—not 
people.  When we tell fortune to people, they want our advice and 
council,  and  they  begin  to  lean  on  us  instead  of  God.    The  Bible  
says tells us; 
• Proverbs  3:5-6    Trust  in  Jehovah  with  all  thy  heart,  And  lean  not  
upon thine own understanding:  In all thy ways acknowledge him, 
And he will direct thy paths.
  
• Psalm  1:1-2  Blessed  is  the man  that  walketh  not  in  the  counsel  
of the wicked, Nor standeth in the way of sinners, Nor sitteth in the 
seat of scoffers: But his delight is in the law of Jehovah
; And on 
his law doth he meditate day and night.  
These  scriptures  tell  us  that  we  should  acknowledge  God  and  
Meditate  on  his  word.    These  passages  emphasize  that  we  are  to  
take our advice first and foremost from him, and if we do this, God 
promises to direct our paths and to bless us. 
An idol is something you put in front of God, and when you do 
this, the thing or person you put in front of God becomes your god.  
This is how you can become a God to others.  If you give them the

Chapter 1: 
Becoming Their God. 
 
The Bible says in Exodus 20:2-3  “I am Jehovah thy God, who 
brought thee out of the land of Egypt, out of the house of bondage.  
Thou  shalt  have  no  other  gods  before  me.”  When  you  start  
telling  fortune  to  people,  they  begin  calling  and  placing  their  
trust in you, but they are supposed to call and place their trust 
in God.  We should take advice from God first and foremost—not 
people.  When we tell fortune to people, they want our advice and 
council,  and  they  begin  to  lean  on  us  instead  of  God.    The  Bible  
says tells us; 
• Proverbs  3:5-6    Trust  in  Jehovah  with  all  thy  heart,  And  lean  not  
upon thine own understanding:  In all thy ways acknowledge him, 
And he will direct thy paths.
  
• Psalm  1:1-2  Blessed  is  the
 man  that  walketh  not  in  the  counsel  
of the wicked, Nor standeth in the way of sinners, Nor sitteth in the 
seat of scoffers: But his delight is in the law of Jehovah
; And on 
his law doth he meditate day and night.  
These  scriptures  tell  us  that  we  should  acknowledge  God  and  
Meditate  on  his  word.    These  passages  emphasize  that  we  are  to  
take our advice first and foremost from him, and if we do this, God 
promises to direct our paths and to bless us. 
An idol is something you put in front of God, and when you do 
this, the thing or person you put in front of God becomes your god.  
This is how you can become a God to others.  If you give them the 

10 
 
answers they are looking for, and they continue to come to you for 
guidance,  then  you  are  becoming  their  god  and  they  are  putting  
you before the one true God.  Jesus said John 14:6  "I am the way, 
the  truth,  and  the  life;  no  one  goes  to  the  Father  except  by  me.”  
The  fortuneteller  tells  the  customer  that  they  have  to  go  through  
her, and then she will go to the higher power on their behalf.  The 
fortuneteller  is  obviously  in  front  of  God,  meaning  they  have  
become a god or an idol.  One of Webster’s Definitions for Idol is; 
a  person  loved  and  honored  to  adoration.    
This  is  also  a  good  
definition of Worship.  Mediate on these scriptures; 
• Leviticus  19:4    Turn  ye  not  unto  idols,  nor  make  to  yourselves  
molten gods: I am Jehovah your God.  
• Exodus  34:14    for  thou  shalt  Worship  no  other  god:  for  Jehovah,  
whose name is Jealous, is a jealous God:  
• 1Corinthians  6:9-10    Surely  you  know  that  the  wicked  will  not  
possess  God's  Kingdom.  Do  not  fool  yourselves;  people  who  are  
immoral or  who  worship  idols  or  are  adulterers  or  homosexual  
perverts  or  who  steal 
or  are  greedy  or  are  drunkards  or  who  
slander  others  or  are  thieves---none  of  these  will  possess  God's  
Kingdom.  
Think  of  the  implications  here.  First,  you  are  making  the  
customer commit idolatry.  What does this mean for the customer?  
It means death.  Do you really desire to cause their death?  Second, 
you  are  keeping  them  from  knowing  the  one  True  God  and  the  
True  Jesus.    Finally  you  keep  them  and  yourself from  a  true  
HEAVEN!!!    You  might  respond  by  saying,  “I  tell  them  not  to  
10 
 
answers they are looking for, and they continue to come to you for 
guidance,  then  you  are  becoming  their  god  and  they  are  putting  
you before the one true God.  Jesus said John 14:6  "I am the way, 
the  truth,  and  the  life;  no  one  goes  to  the  Father  except  by  me.”  
The  fortuneteller  tells  the  customer  that  they  have  to  go  through  
her, and then she will go to the higher power on their behalf.  The 
fortuneteller  is  obviously  in  front  of  God,  meaning  they  have  
become a god or an idol.  One of Webster’s Definitions for Idol is; 
a  person  loved  and  honored  to  adoration.    
This  is  also  a  good  
definition of Worship.  Mediate on these scriptures; 
• Leviticus  19:4    Turn  ye  not  unto  idols,  nor  make  to  yourselves  
molten gods: I am Jehovah your God.  
• Exodus  34:14    for  thou  shalt  Worship  no  other  god:  for  Jehovah,  
whose name is Jealous, is a jealous God:  
• 1Corinthians  6:9-10    Surely  you  know  that  the  wicked  will  not  
possess  God's  Kingdom.  Do  not  fool  yourselves;  people  who  are  
immoral or  who  worship  idols  or  are  adulterers  or  homosexual  
perverts  or  who  steal 
or  are  greedy  or  are  drunkards  or  who  
slander  others  or  are  thieves---none  of  these  will  possess  God's  
Kingdom.  
Think  of  the  implications  here.  First,  you  are  making  the  
customer commit idolatry.  What does this mean for the customer?

12 
 
Chapter 2 
Helping for a Price 
All fortune tellers claim they are helping their customers, but listen 
to what Jesus says in Mat 10:7-8  Go and preach, 'The Kingdom of 
heaven  is  near!'  Heal  the  sick,  bring  the  dead  back  to  life,  heal  
those   who   suffer   from   dreaded   skin   diseases,   and   drive   out   
demons. You have received without paying, so give without being 
paid.   Jesus  was  telling  His  disciples  to  go  and  help  people  
without  being  paid  for  their  services.    Jesus  also  said  “do  unto  
others as you would want them to do to you.” The truth is free; we 
do not pay for it.  Jesus also tells us who The True Helper is...  
• John 14:25-26  "I have told you this while I am still with you.  The 
Helper,  the  Holy  Spirit,  whom  the  Father  will  send  in  my  name,  
will  teach  you  everything  and  make  you  remember  all  that  I  have  
told you.  
• John 16:7  But I am telling you the truth: it is better for you that I 
go away, because if I do not go, The Helper will not come to you. 
But if I do go away, then I will send him to you.  
What about Pastors, why do they get paid?   
• 1Timothy 5:17-18  The elders who do good work as leaders should 
be  considered  worthy  of  receiving  double  pay,  especially  those  
who work hard at preaching and teaching. For the scripture says, 
"Do  not  muzzle  an  ox  when  you  are  using  it  to  thresh  grain"  and  
"Workers should be given their pay."

13 
 
Pastors get paid because they are preaching the true Word of God 
and instructing God’s people so the people may learn to be faithful 
to the  true  Helper.    The  true  Helper  is  The  Holy  Spirit,  and  the  
Holy Spirit is given to those who repent and obey.  The Bible says 
in Acts: 
• Acts  5:30-32    The  God  of  our  ancestors  raised  Jesus  from  death,  
after you had killed him by nailing him to a cross. God raised him 
to his right side as Leader and Savior, to give the people of Israel 
the  opportunity  to  repent  and  have  their  sins  forgiven.  We  are  
witnesses  to  these  things---we  and  the  Holy  Spirit,  who  is  God's  
gift to those who obey him."  
If  you  truly  want  to  help  your  customers,  tell  them  about  Jesus.    
Tell  them  to  go  to  church,  read  the  Bible,  obey  God,  and  repent  
(which  means  to  turn  from  sin).    If  they  really  need  help,  point  
them  to  God.    He  will  give  the  True  Helper—the  Holy  Spirit.    
Then, tell them that you cannot help them anymore.   
If you tell fortune, you need to repent and turn to God, because it is 
very serious.  The things you tell people are false and made up, and 
you charge a price for it.  Listen what the Bible says: 
• 2Peter 2:3-4 In their greed these false teachers
 will make a profit 
out  of  telling  you  made-up  stories.  For  a  long  time  now  their  
Judge  has  been  ready,  and  their  Destroyer  has  been  wide  awake!  
God  did  not  spare  the  angels  who  sinned,  but  threw  them  into  
hell, where they are kept chained in darkness, waiting for the Day 
of Judgment.

Chapter 3 
Advice that comes true 
Why are millions of people calling upon Psychics Today? 
“Founded  in  1990,  the  Psychic  Friends  Network  brings  in  about  
$100  million  per  year,”  said  Richard  Dworman,  editor  of  the  
Infomercial  Marketing  Report,  a  monthly  industry  publication.    
Millions  of  people  are  paying  psychics,  watching  physic  TV  
programs,  chatting  with  psychics  on  the  internet,  and  calling  
psychic  lines.    There  was  a  King  in  the  Bible  who  fell  into  this  
same  trap,  King  Saul.    He  began  his  ministry  in  obedient  to  God,  
but then became jealous and bitter when he stopped listening to the 
prophet  God  had  sent  to  him.    King  Saul  became  disobedient  and  
rebellious toward God, so God abandoned him and took His spirit 
from him. 
• 1 Samuel 16:14  The LORD's spirit left Saul, and an evil spirit sent 
by the LORD tormented him.  
King  Saul  was  in  a  situation  where  he  needed  help,  and  he  knew  
God was not with him anymore because of his disobedience.  Out 
of desperation, Saul went to a fortuneteller, one of the last fortune 
tellers   in   the   land   because   King   Saul   had   thrown   all   the   
fortunetellers out when he became King.  Now, in the height of his 
disobedience, he needed a fortuneteller.  King Saul was desperate, 
and  needed  to  get  in  contact  with  the  prophet  Samuel.    The  
problem  is  that  the  prophet  Samuel  was  dead.    So  in  a  desperate  
15 
 
Chapter 3 
Advice that comes true 
Why are millions of people calling upon Psychics Today? 
“Founded  in  1990,  the  Psychic  Friends  Network  brings  in  about  
$100  million  per  year,”  said  Richard  Dworman,  editor  of  the  
Infomercial  Marketing  Report,  a  monthly  industry  publication.    
Millions  of  people  are  paying  psychics,  watching  physic  TV  
programs,  chatting  with  psychics  on  the  internet,  and  calling  
psychic  lines.    There  was  a  King  in  the  Bible  who  fell  into  this  
same  trap,  King  Saul.    He  began  his  ministry  in  obedient  to  God,  
but then became jealous and bitter when he stopped listening to the 
prophet  God  had  sent  to  him.    King  Saul  became  disobedient  and  
rebellious toward God, so God abandoned him and took His spirit 
from him. 
• 1 Samuel 16:14  The LORD's spirit left Saul, and an evil spirit sent 
by the LORD tormented him.  
King  Saul  was  in  a  situation  where  he  needed  help,  and  he  knew  
God was not with him anymore because of his disobedience.  Out 
of desperation, Saul went to a fortuneteller, one of the last fortune 
tellers   in   the   land   because   King   Saul   had   thrown   all   the   
fortunetellers out when he became King.  Now, in the height of his 
disobedience, he needed a fortuneteller.  King Saul was desperate, 
and  needed  to  get  in  contact  with  the  prophet  Samuel.    The  
problem  is  that  the  prophet  Samuel  was  dead.    So  in  a  desperate  
15

Chapter 3 
Advice that comes true 
Why are millions of people calling upon Psychics Today? 
“Founded  in  1990,  the  Psychic  Friends  Network  brings  in  about  
$100  million  per  year,”  said  Richard  Dworman,  editor  of  the  
Infomercial  Marketing  Report,  a  monthly  industry  publication.    
Millions  of  people  are  paying  psychics,  watching  physic  TV  
programs,  chatting  with  psychics  on  the  internet,  and  calling  
psychic  lines.    There  was  a  King  in  the  Bible  who  fell  into  this  
same  trap,  King  Saul.    He  began  his  ministry  in  obedient  to  God,  
but then became jealous and bitter when he stopped listening to the 
prophet  God  had  sent  to  him.    King  Saul  became  disobedient  and  
rebellious toward God, so God abandoned him and took His spirit 
from him. 
• 1 Samuel 16:14  The LORD's spirit left Saul, and an evil spirit sent 
by the LORD tormented him.  
King  Saul  was  in  a  situation  where  he  needed  help,  and  he  knew  
God was not with him anymore because of his disobedience.  Out 
of desperation, Saul went to a fortuneteller, one of the last fortune 
tellers   in   the   land   because   King   Saul   had   thrown   all   the   
fortunetellers out when he became King.  Now, in the height of his 
disobedience, he needed a fortuneteller.  King Saul was desperate, 
and  needed  to  get  in  contact  with  the  prophet  Samuel.    The  
problem  is  that  the  prophet  Samuel  was  dead.    So  in  a  desperate  

16 
 
attempt,  King  Saul  went  to  a  fortune  teller  to  try  and  contact  
Samuel from the dead. 
• 1Samuel  28:8-16    So  Saul  disguised  himself;  he  put  on  different  
clothes,  and  after  dark  he  went  with  two  of  his  men  to  see  the  
woman.  "Consult  the  spirits  for  me  and  tell  me  what  is  going  to  
happen,"  he  said  to  her.  "Call  up  the  spirit  of  the  man  I  name."  
The  woman  answered,  "Surely  you  know  what  King  Saul  has  
done,  how  he  forced  the  fortunetellers  and  mediums  to  leave 
Israel.  Why,  then,  are  you  trying  to  trap  me  and  get  me  killed?"    
Then Saul made a sacred vow. "By the living LORD I promise that 
you will not be punished for doing this," he told her.  "Whom shall 
I  call  up  for  you?"  the  woman  asked.  "Samuel,"  he  answered.      
When  the  woman  saw  Samuel,  she  screamed  and  said  to  Saul,  
"Why  have  you  tricked  me?  You  are  King  Saul!"    "Don't  be  
afraid!"  the  king  said  to  her.  "What  do  you  see?"  "I  see  a  spirit  
coming  up  from  the  earth,"  she  answered.    "What  does  it  look  
like?" he asked. "It's an old man coming up," she answered. "He is 
wearing  a  cloak."  Then  Saul  knew  that  it  was  Samuel,  and  he  
bowed  to  the  ground  in  respect.    Samuel  said  to  Saul,  "Why  have  
you  disturbed  me?  Why  did  you  make  me  come  back?"  Saul  
answered,  "I  am  in  great  trouble!  The  Philistines  are  at  war  with  
me, and God has abandoned me. He doesn't answer me any more, 
either by prophets or by dreams. And so I have called you, for you 
to  tell  me  what  I  must  do."      Samuel  said,  "Why  do  you  call  me  
when  the  LORD  has  abandoned  you  and  become  your  enemy?   
16

17 
 
The  LORD  has  done  to  you  what  he  told  you  through  me:  he  has  
taken the kingdom away from you and given it to David instead.  
God allowed Samuel to come back in order to show Saul how God 
had  become  his  enemy  because  of  his  disobedience.    God  had  
given  his  position  to  David,  a  man  who  loved  God  and  would  be  
obedient to his word.   How do people become Gods enemy? 
• James  4:2-4    You  want  things,  but  you  cannot  have  them,  so  you  
are  ready  to  kill;  you  strongly  desire  things,  but  you  cannot  get  
them,  so  you  quarrel  and  fight.  You  do  not  have  what  you  want  
because you do not ask God for it.  And when you ask, you do not 
receive it, because your motives are bad; you ask for things to use 
for your own pleasures.   Unfaithful people! Don't you know that 
to be the world's friend means to be God's enemy? If you want to 
be the world's friend, you make yourself God's enemy.  
Now  to  answer  the  question  I  posed  earlier;  why  are  millions  of  
people  seeking  physics  today?    They  are  seeking  out  physics  
because  they  are  disobedient  to  God,  they  do  not  trust  God,  
and  they  have  become  God’s  enemy.    Like  King  Saul,  they  do  
not want to repent!  So they turn to the fortuneteller for hope, 
but  just  like  this  fortune  teller  could  not  help  King  Saul,  they  
will never be helped.  God commanded the people of Israel not to 
go  to  fortunetellers  for  advice,  because  He  would  send  them  
someone ordained by him to advise them. 
• Deuteronomy  18:9-15    When  you  have  come  into  the  land  which  
the Lord your God is giving you, do not take as your example the 
disgusting ways of those nations. Let there not be seen among you

18 
 
anyone who makes his son or his daughter go through the fire, or 
anyone  using  secret  arts,
  or  a  maker  of  strange  sounds,  or  a  
reader  of  signs,  or  any  wonder-worker,  Or  anyone  using  secret  
force  on  people,  or  putting  questions  to  a  spirit,  or  having  secret  
knowledge,  or  going  to  the  dead  for  directions.    For  all  who  do  
such  things  are  disgusting  to  the  Lord;  and  because  of  these  
disgusting  things  the  Lord  your  God  is  driving  them  out  before  
you. You are to be upright in heart before the Lord your God. For 
these nations, whose land you are taking, give attention to readers 
of signs and to those using secret arts: but the Lord your God will 
not  let  you  do  so.    The  Lord  your  God  will  give  you  a  prophet  
from among your people, like me; you will give ear to him;  
The name Samuel means “Heard of by God,” and only leaders who 
are telling Gods true Word and ordained by God can advise people 
how  to  live  a  life  that  Pleases  God.    This  is  why  God  has  given  
the  people  pastors,  shepherd  or  leaders  to  give  the  people  
God’s word and God’s word alone. 
 
Have you ever given advice or predicted something for one of your 
customers  and  it  came  true?    The  answer is probably a yes.  Why 
did those things come true? 
• Ephesians 2:1-2  And you were dead in the trespasses and sins in 
which   you   once   walked,   following   the   course   of   this   world,   
following the prince of the power of the air, the spirit that is now 
at work in the sons of disobedience

God’s word and God’s word alone. 
 
Have you ever given advice or predicted something for one of your 
customers  and  it  came  true?    The  answer is probably a yes.  Why 
did those things come true? 
• Ephesians 2:1-2  And you were dead in the trespasses and sins in 
which   you   once   walked,   following   the   course   of   this   world,   
following the prince of the power of the air
, the spirit that is now 
at work in the sons of disobedience 
18 
 
anyone who makes his son or his daughter go through the fire, or 
anyone  using  secret  arts,
  or  a  maker  of  strange  sounds,  or  a  
reader  of  signs,  or  any  wonder-worker,  Or  anyone  using  secret  
force  on  people,  or  putting  questions  to  a  spirit,  or  having  secret  
knowledge,  or  going  to  the  dead  for  directions.    For  all  who  do  
such  things  are  disgusting  to  the  Lord
;  and  because  of  these  
disgusting  things  the  Lord  your  God  is  driving  them  out  before  
you. You are to be upright in heart before the Lord your God. For 
these nations, whose land you are taking, give attention to readers 
of signs and to those using secret arts: but the Lord your God will 
not  let  you  do  so.    The  Lord  your  God  will  give  you  a  prophet  
from among your people, like me; you will give ear to him;  
The name Samuel means “Heard of by God,” and only leaders who 
are telling Gods true Word and ordained by God can advise people 
how  to  live  a  life  that  Pleases  God.    This  is  why  God  has  given  
the  people  pastors,  shepherd  or  leaders  to  give  the  people  
God’s word and God’s word alone. 
 
Have you ever given advice or predicted something for one of your 
customers  and  it  came  true?    The  answer is probably a yes.  Why 
did those things come true? 
• Ephesians 2:1-2  And you were dead in the trespasses and sins in 
which   you   once   walked,   following   the   course   of   this   world,   
following the prince of the power of the air
, the spirit that is now 
at work in the sons of disobedience 

19 
 
The  prince  of  the  power  of  the  air  is  the  Devil,  and  when  we  are  
disobedient to God we are under the influence of the devil.  Have 
you  ever  wondered  how  you  predicted  something  that  came  true?    
Of  course  you  have.    The  reason  you  have  is  because  you  are  in  
direct disobedience to God, and so the devil has the rights to put it 
in your head.  You then repeat what the Devil says, and the Devil 
is working in you because you are in disobedience.  You might say 
how  am  I  disobedient  to  God?    You  are  disobedient  to  God  when  
you  tell  fortune,  because  when  you  tell  fortune  you  are  using  
sorcery, lying, and idolatry. 
• Revelations  21:8    But  as  for  the  cowardly,  the  faithless,  the  
detestable,  as  for  murderers,  the  sexually  immoral,  sorcerers, 
idolaters, and all liars their portion will be in the lake that burns 
with fire and sulfur, which is the second death."    
You  are  not  only  being  disobedient,  but  you  are  causing  those  
people   who   come   to   you   for   advice   to   also   be   in   direct   
disobedience to God.  The Bible tells us plainly;  
• Leviticus   19:26"Never   cast   evil   spells,   and   never   consult   
fortunetellers.  
  Now  the  enemy  is  working  through  both  of  you;  the  thing  you  
have  predicted  to  the  customer  has  come  true  because  of  both  of  
your  disobedience  to  God,  and  the  devil  will  continue  making  it  
come  true  to  keep  the  customer  from  leaving  you  and  fortune  
telling   behind.      Basically,    Satan’s    plans    are    to    keep    the    
disobedience  going  in  both  your  lives  until  it  is  too  late...God  
comes back or one of the two die.  If we are living in disobedience 
19

And  of  course,  everyone  is  a  sinner  and  everyone  makes  
mistakes, but there is a huge difference between making a mistake 
and tripping, and falling on purpose.  The very definition of sin is a 
willful  act  done  against  God  and  his  law,  and  if  you  die  in  your  
sins,  you  will  spend  eternity  in  Hell  and  not  Heaven.    Jesus’  first  
message  was  to  repent  (turn  from  your  sin)  for  the  kingdom  of  
God is at hand. 
• Hebrews  10:26-27    For  there  is  no  longer  any  sacrifice  that  will  
take  away  sins  if  we  purposely  go  on  sinning  after  the  truth  has  
been  made  known  to  us.  Instead,  all  that  is  left  is  to  wait  in  fear  
for  the  coming  Judgment  and  the  fierce  fire  which  will  destroy  
those who oppose God!

21 
 
were slaves. Such people are evil and are trying to lead you away 
from  the  life  that  the  LORD  has  commanded  you  to  live.  They  
must be put to death, in order to rid yourselves of this evil.  
God  is  clearly  saying  in  these  passages  that  the  people  who  give  
this advice must be put to death.  Why?  Because God is a jealous 
God that does not want His people to listen to anyone but Him, and 
he knows that the enemy will only lead people away from the real 
truth.    God  alone  has  the  words  and  the  ways  to  righteousness.    
The  advice  fortune  tellers  give  is  not  advice
  to  repent,  turn  from  
sins, and worship God alone.  If we do not listen to God and God 
alone,  then  we  do  not  live  a  life  God  desires  us  to  live.    God  will  
destroy any person who would try to mislead His People...and after 
death, comes the second death.  
• Revelation  20:14    Then  death  and  the  world  of  the  dead  were  
thrown  into  the  lake  of  fire.  Those  who  did  not  have  their  name  
written in the book of the living were thrown into the lake of fire.

fortunetellers.

The  “advice”  that  you  give  the  customer  is  against  God...  even  
though  it  might  come  true.    You  are  not  helping,  but  you  are

Wonderful Counselor for true Advice,  
• Isaiah 9:6  A child is born to us! A son is given to us! And he will 
be  our  ruler.  He  will  be  called,  "Wonderful  Counselor,"  "Mighty  
God," "Eternal Father," "Prince of Peace."  
That is Jesus Christ, the Living Son of God.  He is the Wonderful 
Counselor. 
You too need to stop giving advice, and take some advice from the 
Wonderful counselor

Chapter 4 
Using the Church. 
Many fortune tellers use the Church, the name of Jesus, and God to 
get the people to trust them.  They might say things like, “we will 
give the money to the Church” or things of that nature that make 
the customer believe he or she is actually a servant of God.  Read 
what happened to a couple that lied about giving money to the 
church. 
• Acts 5:1-5  But there was a man named Ananias, who with his wife 
Sapphira  sold  some  property  that  belonged  to  them.    But  with  his  
wife's agreement he kept part of the money for himself and turned 
the rest over to the apostles.  Peter said to him, "Ananias, why did 
you  let  Satan  take  control  of  you  and  make  you  lie  to  the  Holy  
Spirit by keeping part of the money you received for the property?  
Before you sold the property, it belonged to you; and after you sold 
it,  the  money  was  yours.  Why,  then,  did  you  decide  to  do  such  a  
thing? You  have  not  lied  to  people---you  have  lied  to  God!”  As 
soon as Ananias heard this, he fell down dead; and all who heard 
about it were terrified. 
Basically this  couple  said,  “let’s  keep  some  of  the  money  we  got  
from the property we sold, and we will say the rest is actually the 
full amount we received from the property.”  They did this so they 
could look good in front of the Disciples.  The end result was that 
both of them died for lying.  They did not have to give the money 
to  the  church,  they  were  not  pressured  into  doing  it,  but  they  
wanted  to  look  good.    They  thought  they  could  deceive  the  
23

24 
 
Disciples, but what they failed to realize is that they were not lying 
to the disciples, but they were lying to God.  If you use the church 
to  help  push  your  own  agenda,  you  might  not  die  physically,  but  
you will definitely die spiritually.  
 
You  might  say,  “I  never  claimed  to  give  the  money  to  the  
churches,” but do you use Gods name at all? 
• Exodus  20:7    "Do  not  use  my  name  for  evil  purposes,  for  I,  the  
LORD your God, will punish anyone who misuses my name.  
Do not use the name of God at all, because God will punish you. 
 
Some  fortune  tellers  say  things  like  “I  Pray  with  them,  I  read  the  
Bible   to   them,   I   bring   them   to   church.”      Well,   read   these   
scriptures... 
• Isaiah 59:1-4  Don't think that the LORD is too weak to save you 
or  too  deaf  to  hear  your  call  for  help!    It  is  because  of  your  sins  
that  he  doesn't  hear  you.  It  is  your  sins  that  separate  you  from  
God  when  you  try  to  worship  him.  You  are  guilty  of  lying,  
violence, and murder. You go to court, but you do not have justice 
on your side. You depend on lies to win your case. You  carry  out  
your plans to hurt others.  
• Jeremiah  9:5-9    Everyone  deceives  his  neighbor,  and  no  one  
speaks  the  truth;  they  have  taught  their  tongue  to  speak  lies;  they  
weary  themselves  committing  iniquity.  Heaping  oppression  upon  
oppression,  and  deceit  upon  deceit,  they  refuse  to  know  me,  
declares  the  LORD.    Therefore  thus  says  the  LORD  of  hosts:

25 
 
"Behold,  I  will  refine  them  and  test  them,  for  what  else  can  I  do,  
because  of  my  people?    Their  tongue  is  a  deadly  arrow;  it  speaks  
deceitfully; with his mouth each speaks peace to his neighbor, but 
in  his  heart  he  plans  an  ambush  for  him.  Shall  I  not  punish  
them for these things? declares the LORD, and shall I not avenge 
myself on a nation such as this?  
The fortune teller acts like they care for people, but really they 
deceive their concern is for themselves.  People who use God’s 
word, but do not have it hidden in their heart are setting up an 
ambush  for  themselves.    This  is  what  the  devil  did  in  the  
Garden of Eden; he twisted the words of God to get Adam and 
Eve to sin.  If you do this, God says he will punish you. 
• Jeremiah  23:31-32    I  am  also  against  those  prophets  who  speak  
their own words and claim they came from me.   Listen to what I, 
the  LORD,  say!  I  am  against  the  prophets  who  tell  their  dreams  
that  are  full  of  lies.  They  tell  these  dreams  and  lead  my  people  
astray  with  their  lies  and  their  boasting.  I  did  not  send  them  or  
order  them  to  go,  and  they  are  of  no  help  at  all  to  the  people.  I,  
the LORD, have spoken."  
 
Another  important  point  is  that  some  “so  called  Christians”  know  
the  truth  about  what  God  wants,  but  does  not  tell  the  people  
because of greed.  God’s anger will come upon these people.

the  sin  and  evil  of  the  people  whose  evil  ways  prevent  the  truth

from  being  known. God  punishes  them,  because  what  can  be  
known about God is plain to them, for God himself made it plain.  
It’s not just about praying with them, reading the Bible to them, or 
taking them to church, but it is about doing all of these things with 
no  hidden  agenda  and  without  charging  them  money  and  acting  
like they need you to do it.  Listen to what God tells people who do 
not take His name seriously; 
• Malachi  2:2    If  you  will  not  give  ear  and  take  it  to  heart,  to  give  
glory  to  my  name,  says  the  Lord  of  armies,  then  I  will  send  the  
curse  on  you  and  will  put  a  curse  on  your  blessing:  truly,  even  
now I have put a curse on it, because you do not take it to heart.  
It is time to get your heart right with God.   
 
There was a man named Simon who used witchcraft, sorcery, and 
fortune  telling  to  amaze  the  people,  and  many  people  thought  it  
was  the  power  of  God.    Jesus’  Disciples  came  to  his  region  
preaching and teaching the true word of God, and Simon Believed 
in  the  name  of  Jesus  and  got  baptized,  but  Simon  still  did  not  
receive the Holy Spirit.  Why was this? Because Simon’s heart was 
not  right  with  God.    Simon  believed  in  the  miracles  the  disciples  
where  performing  more  then  he  believed  in  the  Master,  Jesus  
Christ.    Simon  even  followed  the  disciples  around,  hung  out  with  
them,  and  ate  with  them.    Simon  even  offered  money  to  the  
disciples if they could give him this power he seen manifesting in 
their lives.  Read what happened: 
26

• Malachi  2:2    If  you  will  not  give  ear  and  take  it  to  heart,  to  give  
glory  to  my  name,  says  the  Lord  of  armies,  then  I  will  send  the  
curse  on  you  and  will  put  a  curse  on  your  blessing:  truly,  even  
now I have put a curse on it, because you do not take it to heart.  
It is time to get your heart right with God.   
 
There was a man named Simon who used witchcraft, sorcery, and 
fortune  telling  to  amaze  the  people,  and  many  people  thought  it  
was  the  power  of  God.    Jesus’  Disciples  came  to  his  region  
preaching and teaching the true word of God, and Simon Believed 
in  the  name  of  Jesus  and  got  baptized,  but  Simon  still  did  not  
receive the Holy Spirit.  Why was this? Because Simon’s heart was 
not  right  with  God.    Simon  believed  in  the  miracles  the  disciples  
where  performing  more  then  he  believed  in  the  Master,  Jesus  
Christ.    Simon  even  followed  the  disciples  around,  hung  out  with  
them,  and  ate  with  them.    Simon  even  offered  money  to  the  
disciples if they could give him this power he seen manifesting in 
their lives.  Read what happened: 
26 
 
from  being  known. God  punishes  them,  because  what  can  be  
known about God is plain to them, for God himself made it plain.  
It’s not just about praying with them, reading the Bible to them, or 
taking them to church, but it is about doing all of these things with 
no  hidden  agenda  and  without  charging  them  money  and  acting  
like they need you to do it.  Listen to what God tells people who do 
not take His name seriously;

• Acts  8:11-24    For  a  long  time,  Simon  had  used  witchcraft  to  
amaze the people, and they kept crowding around him.  But when 
they  believed  what  Philip  was  saying  about  God's  kingdom  and  
about  the  name  of  Jesus  Christ,  they  were  all  baptized.    Even 
Simon  believed  and  was  baptized.  He  stayed  close  to  Philip,  
because  he  marveled  at  all  the  miracles  and  wonders.    The 
apostles  in  Jerusalem  heard  that  some  people  in  Samaria  had  
accepted God's message, and they sent Peter and John.  When the 
two  apostles  arrived,  they  prayed  that  the  people  would  be  given  
the Holy Spirit.  Before this, the Holy Spirit had not been given to 
anyone in Samaria, though some of them had been baptized in the 
name  of  the  Lord  Jesus.    Peter  and  John  then  placed  their  hands  
on  everyone  who  had  faith  in  the  Lord,  and  they  were  given  the  
Holy  Spirit.    Simon  noticed  that  the  Spirit  was  given  only  when  
the  apostles  placed  their  hands  on  the  people.  So  he  brought  
money  and said to Peter and John, "Let me have this power too! 
Then  anyone  I  place  my  hands  on  will  also  be  given  the  Holy  
Spirit."    Peter  said  to  him,  "You  and  your  money  will  both  end  
up  in  hell  if  you  think  you  can  buy  God's  gift!    You  don't  have  
any part in this, and God sees that your heart isn't right.  Get rid 
of these evil thoughts and ask God to forgive you.  I can see that 
you  are  jealous  and  bound  by  your  evil  ways."    Simon  said,  
"Please pray to the Lord, so that what you said won't happen to 
me."  
Simon  desired  to  use  the  church  for  his  own  personal  gain.  He  
claimed  he  wanted  to  help  others,  but  he  really  needed  help  
27

28 
 
himself.  He desired to give the Holy Spirit to other people, but he 
did  not  even  have  the  Holy  Spirit  in  his  own  life!    Fortune  tellers  
say  they  want  to  help  people,  and  they  want  to  use  God’s  name  
while  doing  it...  but  every  one  of  them  need  help  themselves.    
They   say   things   to   their   customers   like,   “your   husband   or   
boyfriend is not good to you,” or “people in your life are not your 
true friends,” or “you are smiling on the outside, but you are really 
miserable on the inside.”  The fortune teller is telling the customer 
that they can help them with all these things, when they really need 
help  themselves...and  they  cannot  even  help  themselves.      Only  
the Holy Spirit can produce true happiness in people’s lives.   
• Galatians 5:22-26  God's Spirit makes us loving, happy, peaceful, 
patient,  kind,  good,  faithful,  gentle,  and  self-controlled.  There  is  
no  law  against  behaving  in  any  of  these  ways.    And  because  we  
belong  to  Christ  Jesus,  we  have  killed  our  selfish  feelings  and  
desires.    God's  Spirit  has  given  us  life,  and  so  we  should  follow  
the  Spirit.  But  don't  be  conceited  or  make  others  jealous  by  
claiming to be better than they are.  
Fortune  tellers  say  they  can  produce  these  benefits  in  people’s  
lives,  but  the  truth  is  they  cannot  even  produce  them  in  their  own  
lives. They claim to do what only the Holy Spirit can do, and they 
do not even have the Holy Spirit!  How can I say this? 
• Acts  5:30-32    The  God  of  our  ancestors  raised  Jesus  from  death,  
after you had killed him by nailing him to a cross. God raised him 
to his right side as Leader and Savior, to give the people of Israel 
the  opportunity  to  repent  and  have  their  sins  forgiven.  We  are  
28 
 
himself.  He desired to give the Holy Spirit to other people, but he

witnesses  to  these  things---we  and  the  Holy  Spirit,  who  is  God's  
gift to those who obey him." 
• Titus 3:5  he saved us. It was not because of any good deeds that 
we ourselves had done, but because of his own mercy that he saved 
us, through the Holy Spirit, who gives us new birth and new life 
by washing us.  
 
Are you obeying God? Do you have a new life? Have you been 
cleansed by God?  Have you stoped your old ways? 
 
Notice that right before the fruit of the spirit is mentioned, the fruit 
of the flesh is mentioned. 
• Galatians  5:19-21    What  human  nature  does  is  quite  plain.  It  
shows itself in immoral, filthy, and indecent actions; in worship of 
idols and witchcraft.  People  become  enemies  and  they  fight;  they  
become  jealous,  angry,  and  ambitious.  They  separate  into  parties  
and groups; they are envious, get drunk, have orgies, and do other 
things like these. I warn you now as I have before: those who do 
these things will not possess the Kingdom of God.  
If you have these things present in your life, what makes you think 
you  have  Gods  Holy  Spirit  in  your  life?    Stop  trying  to  use  the  
church  to  further  your  own  agenda.  Stop  saying  you  have  “gifts”  
when  you  know  you  do  not.    Stop  saying  you  can  help  people,  
when  you  cannot  even  help  yourself.    Stop  using  pictures  and  
statues to make people believe you are a Christian.  Simon tried to 
use the Church for his own corrupt ways and he was warned what 
29 
 
witnesses  to  these  things---we  and  the  Holy  Spirit,  who  is  God's  
gift to those who obey him."

shows itself in immoral, filthy, and indecent actions; in worship of 
idols 
and witchcraft.  People  become  enemies  and  they  fight;  they  
become  jealous,  angry
,  and  ambitious.  They  separate  into  parties  
and groups; they are envious, get drunk,
 have orgies, and do other 
things like these. I warn you now as I have before: those who do 
these things will not possess the Kingdom of God.  
If you have these things present in your life, what makes you think 
you  have  Gods  Holy  Spirit  in  your  life?    Stop  trying  to  use  the  
church  to  further  your  own  agenda.  Stop  saying  you  have  “gifts”  
when  you  know  you  do  not.    Stop  saying  you  can  help  people,  
when  you  cannot  even  help  yourself.    Stop  using  pictures  and  
statues to make people believe you are a Christian.  Simon tried to 
use the Church for his own corrupt ways and he was warned what 

30 
 
would happen if he did not change.  If you do not stop, the Bible is 
very  clear  about  what  will  happen  to  you.    You  too  are  being  
warned.   
• Act  8:20-21  Peter  said  to  him,  "You  and  your  money  will  both  
end  up  in  hell  if  you  think  you  can  buy  God's  gift! You  don't  
have any part in this, and God sees that your heart isn't right.  
Money  cannot  buy  God  or  the  gift  of  the  Holy  Spirit.    Only  
obedience,  repentance  of  sin,  and  complete  submission  is  how  we  
receive this wonderful gift. 
• Act 8:22-23  Get rid of these evil thoughts and ask God to forgive 
you. I can see that you are jealous and bound by your evil ways."  
 
Instead of using the church, start going to church.  Instead of trying 
to help others, go get some help.   Simon  had  his  Revelation.      He  
did  not  get  mad.    He  did  not  deny  he  was  wrong.    He  received  it.    
He  received  the  rebuke,  the  correction,  and  he  submitted  and  
changed  his  ways.      Do  not  get  mad  or  upset  if  you  are  feeling  
convicted,  these  are  not  my  words,  just  like  they  were  not  Peter’s  
words  to  Simon.    These  are  the  words  of  God.      Receive  this  
revelation,  and  pray  to  God  to  help  you.    Instead  of  using  the  
church,  start  going  to  church  so  you  can  be  shown  how  to  truly  
follow Gods ways.  Instead of using God’s name in vain, call upon 
His name and repent from your sin
.

Chapter 5 
Witchcraft or just a Scam? 
 
Here  are  a  couple  of  Webster’s  Dictionary  definitions  regarding  
Witchcraft. 
WITCH, n. 1. A woman who by compact with the devil, practices 
sorcery  or  enchantment.  2.  A  woman  who  is  given  to  unlawful  
arts. 
CRAFT,  n.  1.  Art;  ability;  skill.    2.  Cunning,  art  or  skill,  in  a  
bad  sense,  or  applied  to  bad  purposes;  artifice;  guile;  skill  or  
dexterity employed to effect purposes by deceit. 
According   to   these   definitions,   people   who   tell   fortune   are   
obviously   practicing   witchcraft.      First,   they   are   practicing   
unlawful  arts.    Secondly,  they  practice  deceit,  and  their  skills  
are  applied  in  a  bad  sense.    So  ask  yourself,  “Is  fortune  telling  
witchcraft?”    You  might  try  and  argue  the  matter,  and  that  is  
exactly  what  Satan  wants  you  to  do.    He  desires  that  you  stay  
deceived  and  stay  in  your  sins.    The  Bible  calls  him  the  father  of  
all lies and it also says there is no truth in him. 
• John 8:43-45  Why do you not understand what I say? It is because 
you cannot bear to listen to my message.  
You are the children of 
your father, the Devil, and you want to follow your father's desires. 
From the very beginning he was a murderer and has never been on 
the side of truth, because there is no truth in him. When he tells a 
lie, he is only doing what is natural to him, because he is a liar and 
32

Think  about  the  things  you  tell  your  customers  to  do.    I  will  not  
mention any of the tricks and scams, but just think about them for

WITCH, n. 1. A woman who by compact with the devil, practices 
sorcery  or  enchantment.  2.  A  woman  who  is  given  to  unlawful  
arts. 
CRAFT,  n.    1.  Art;  ability;;  skill.    2.  Cunning,  art  or  skill,  in  a  
bad  sense,  or  applied  to  bad  purposes;  artifice;  guile;  skill  or  
dexterity employed to effect purposes by deceit. 
 
Now   read   this   definition   of   both   words   put   together   in   the   
Webster’s dictionary   
WITCHCRAFT,  [witch  and  craft.]    The  practices  of  witches;  
sorcery; enchantments; communication with the devil. 
 
The wording here is very strong—too strong to ignore.  Hopefully 
you  will  understand  that  this  is  not  just  another  scam,  but  it  is  
witchcraft in its purest form.  Whether you accept it or not, you are

doing Satan’s work.  You are hiding the truth, and acting like those 
tricks and charms truly work.  You are keeping people from God. 
 
You  might  still  be  arguing  the  point  that  this  is  not  witchcraft.  
Well, do you agree that stealing and lying is wrong?  Of course it 
is!    If  you  agree  these  things  are  wrong,  and  you  keep  on  doing  
them,  then  you  are  in  rebellion  against  God.    Read  these  two  
scriptures: 
• 1Samuel  15:23  For  rebellion  is  as  the  sin  of  witchcraft,  and  
stubbornness  is  as  idolatry  and  teraphim.  Because  thou  hast  
rejected the word of Jehovah, he hath also rejected thee 
• Gal 5:19  Now the works of the flesh are manifest, which are these; 
Adultery,    fornication,    uncleanness,    lasciviousness,    Idolatry, 
witchcraft,  hatred,  variance,  emulations,  wrath,  strife,  seditions,  
heresies, Envyings,  murders,  drunkenness,  revellings,  and  such  
like: of the which I tell you before, as I have also told you in time 
past, that   they   which   do   such   things   shall   not   inherit   the   
kingdom of God. 
This  is  serious  stuff  with  serious  consequences.    I  pray  that  you  will  
receive the Word of 
God  and  not  reject  it!    If  we  repent,  which  means  “changing  your  
ways, God will forgive 
us.  
• Jeremiah  33:8    I  will  purify  them  from  the  sins  that  they  have  
committed  against  me,  and  I  will  forgive  their  sins  and  their  
rebellion.

• 1Samuel  15:23  For  rebellion  is  as  the  sin  of  witchcraft,  and  
stubbornness  is  as  idolatry  and  teraphim.  Because  thou  hast  
rejected the word of Jehovah, he hath also rejected thee 
• Gal 5:19  Now the works of the flesh are manifest, which are these; 
Adultery,    fornication,    uncleanness,    lasciviousness,    Idolatry, 
witchcraft,  hatred,  variance,  emulations,  wrath,  strife,  seditions,  
heresies, Envyings,  murders,  drunkenness,  revellings,  and  such  
like: of the which I tell you before, as I have also told you in time 
past, that   they   which   do   such   things   shall   not   inherit   the   
kingdom of God. 
This  is  serious  stuff  with  serious  consequences.    I  pray  that  you  will  
receive the Word of 
God  and  not  reject  it!    If  we  repent,  which  means  “changing  your  
ways, God will forgive 
us.  
• Jeremiah  33:8    I  will  purify  them  from  the  sins  that  they  have  
committed  against  me,  and  I  will  forgive  their  sins  and  their  
rebellion.  
34 
 
doing Satan’s work.  You are hiding the truth, and acting like those

Chapter 6 
Uninvited Guests 
When  we  practice  fortune  telling,  which  we  found  out  in  the  
previous   chapters   is   witchcraft,   sorcery,   idolatry,   and   clearly   
Satan's  work...  demons  are  present.    When  my  wife  Gina  told  
fortune,  she  would  sometimes  see  demons  in  human  form.    Many  
people  who  tell  fortune  see  and  sense  Spirits.    Many  people,  
especially fortune tellers, feel that when they see “ghosts,” whether 
they are family or not, they believe it is actually a deceased person 
walking around on earth.  The Bible says: 
• Hebrews  9:27    And  as  it  is  appointed  unto  men  once  to  die,  but  
after this the judgment: 
• 2  Corinthians  11:14    Well,  no  wonder!  Even  Satan  can  disguise  
himself to look like an angel of light!  
When a person dies, he or she will either go directly to Heaven or 
Hell.  If they were walking according to God’s word, they will be 
permitted  into  heaven.    If  they  were  walking  against  the  word  of  
God, they will be sentenced to hell.  When a person dies, they will 
be  judged  for  every  work  they  have  ever  done,  good  and  bad.    
There  is  no  limbo,  no  purgatory,  and  no  wandering  spirits...only  
Heaven or Hell.  Think about it this way, have you or someone you 
know  ever  seen  a  “ghost”  in  child  form?    Many  people  have.  
Children  are  pure...  why  would  God  leave  them  behind?    He  
would not.  He does not.  God does not leave anyone to wander the 
earth,  nor  can  anyone  escape  judgment.    What  people  see  is  not  
“ghosts,”   but   they   are   demonic   spirits   (demons)   disguising   
35

36 
 
themselves  as  people,  adults  or  even  children.    They  are  demons  
disguising  themselves  as  angels  of  light.    It  is  not  your  family  
member or your friend.  It is not the person who lived in the house 
before you and maybe even died there...it is a demon. 
• Ephesians 6:12  For we are not fighting against human beings but 
against  the  wicked  spiritual  forces  in  the  heavenly  world,  the  
rulers, authorities, and cosmic powers of this dark age.  
The Bible tells us that our true fight takes place in the Spirit World 
against  demons.    When  we  disobey  God,  we  are  giving  into  the  
demons and giving them control of our lives.   
 
• Ephesians 2:1-2  In the past you were spiritually dead because of 
your disobedience and sins.  At that time you followed the world's 
evil way; you obeyed the ruler of the spiritual powers in space, the 
spirit who now controls the people who disobey God.  
When  you  are  disobedient  to  God,  the  Spirit  of  Satan  controls  you  
(demons).   
• 1Timothy  4:1-2    The  Spirit  says  clearly  that  some  people  will  
abandon  the  faith  in  later  times;  they  will  obey  lying  spirits  and  
follow the  teachings  of  demons.    Such  teachings  are  spread  by  
deceitful liars, whose consciences are dead, as if burnt with a hot 
iron.  
That is all fortune telling is; deceit, lying and the twisting of the truth. 
Look what King David wrote: 
• Psalms 19:13  Keep me safe, also, from willful sins; don't let them 
rule over me. Then I shall be perfect and free from the evil of sin.

Many People get angry when you tell them they are doing wrong.  
The demon who is influencing them, tells them they are different, 
they  are  exempt,  or  that  whoever  is  pointing  out  their  wrong  is  
judging, condemning, and just picking on them (Satan comes as an 
Angel of light).  They are being deceived by the teaching of these 
demons. 
 
In  the  Book  of  Acts,  the  Bible  talks  about  a  fortuneteller  that  was  
possessed by a demon 
• Acts  16:16-19    One  day  as  we  were  going  to  the  place  of  prayer,  
we were met by a young servant woman who had an evil spirit that 
enabled  her  to  predict  the  future.  She  earned  a  lot  of  money  for  
her   owners   by   telling   fortunes.   She   followed   Paul   and   us,   
shouting,  "These  men  are  servants  of  the  Most  High  God!  They  
announce  to  you  how  you  can  be  saved!"  She  did  this  for  many  
days, until Paul became so upset that he turned around and said to 
the spirit, "In the name of Jesus Christ I order you to come out of 
her!"  The  spirit  went  out  of  her  that  very  moment.    When  her  
owners realized that their chance of making money was gone, they 
seized  Paul  and  Silas  and  dragged  them  to  the  authorities  in  the  
public square.  
The  fortune  teller  was  saying  the  truth;  she  was  saying  that  Paul  
the  apostle  was  going  to  tell  people  how  to  be  saved.    Why  was  
Paul frustrated?  He was frustrated because Satan was trying to get 
glory  for  telling  the  people  in  advance  why  Paul  was  coming.    
Satan  was  trying  to  make  the  fortune  teller  look  good  in  front  of  
37

the  people.    Most  fortune  tellers  mix  the  truth  with  lies,  and  they  
do  it  to  make  themselves  look  good  and  more  credible  in  front  of  
the customer. 
 
Right  about  now  some  of  you  are  getting  angry  because  you  are  
insulted by me saying that you have a demon in your life, but it is 
the  truth.    If  you  are  partaking  in  any  type  of  sin,  you  are  being  
influenced by demons.  There was a time when I had many demons 
in  my  life.    The  demons  influencing  me  were;  adultery,  lying,  
addiction,  rage  and  immorality.    The  truth  of  the  matter  is  that  
people  who  are  disobedient  to  God  are  under  the  influence  of  
demons.  How do you think your predictions come true?  Because 
like  I  said  before,  the  customer  and  the  fortuneteller  are  in  
disobedient  to  God,  so  the  demons  can  make  these  things  come  
true  to  keep  both  the  customer  and  the  fortuneteller  in  bondage.  
The sign you have in front of your house to advertise your business 
is something like, “readings by ________” or “Psychic reader and 
astrologer.”  In the spirit world is an advertisement for Demons 
to come in. 
 
Demons     bring     Sickness,     depression,     fighting,     anger,     
unforgiveness, bitterness, adultery, and sexual immorality.  Are 
any  of  these  things  in  your  life?    If  they  are,  then  listen  to  a  few  
verses about how to destroy the works of Satan in your life. 
• 2Corinthians 10:3-5  It is true that we live in the world, but we do 
not  fight  from  worldly  motives.  The  weapons  we  use  in  our  fight

the  people.    Most  fortune  tellers  mix  the  truth  with  lies,  and  they  
do  it  to  make  themselves  look  good  and  more  credible  in  front  of  
the customer.

39 
 
are not the world's weapons but God's powerful weapons, which 
we  use  to  destroy strong  holds.  We  destroy  false  arguments;  we  
pull   down   every   proud   obstacle   that   is   raised   against   the   
knowledge  of  God;  we  take  every  thought  captive  and  make  it  
obey Christ.  
When  you  feel  like  doing  wrong  or  a  suggestion  of  sin  runs  
through your head, take that thought captive and make it obey God 
by  choosing  to  not  do  wrong,  and  by  asking  Jesus’  help  get  you  
through it! 
• Romans 6:12-13  Sin must no longer rule in your mortal bodies, so 
that  you  obey  the  desires  of  your  natural  self.    Nor  must  you  
surrender  any  part  of  yourselves  to  sin  to  be  used  for  wicked 
purposes. Instead, give yourselves to God, as those who have been 
brought  from  death  to  life,  and  surrender  your  whole  being  to  
him to be used for righteous purposes.  
Give your life fully to God.  Surrender your life to God.  This way 
God can use you to be a Servant of Jesus and no longer a servant 
of  the  devil  and  his  demons.    You  might  say,  “but  I  believe  in  
God”  or  “I  do  Love  God.”    If  you  do,  then  you  need  to  prove  it  
with your actions!  The Bible says; 
• James 2:19-20  Do you believe that there is only one God? Good! 
The  demons  also  believe---and  tremble  with  fear.  You  fool!  Do  
you want to be shown that faith without actions is useless?

Chapter 7 
Your boss behind the scenes 
 
Most  fortune  tellers  believe  they  are  their  own  boss,  but  little  do  
they know that their boss is really Satan.  How?  Well let’s look at 
the facts. 
(1) God  has  ordained  the  use  of  something  called  Christian  tracks.    
These  are  evangelistic  literature  and  pamphlets  that  tell  how  God  
can help you.  Sometimes it will say the address of the church that 
passed them out.  Satan has ordained something for fortune tellers 
called “hand bills.”  This literature tells how the fortune teller can 
help them and the address to their place of business. 
(2) God uses a Building called the Church where people can go to get 
the  true  word  of  God  and  Worship  Him.    Satan  uses  a  building  
fortunetellers  call  an  “offisa,”  where  people  go  to  get  the  lies  of  
Satan, and worship Satan without realizing it. 
(3) God has appointed Pastors who council people in the right ways of 
God.    Satan  has  appointed  fortune  tellers  to  council  people  in  the  
wrong ways of God through Satan 
(4) God has made the Bible to direct people in worshipping and living 
in  the  right  way  so  they  will  be  able  to  live  a  blessed  life  and  
inherit  the  Kingdom  of  God.    Satan  has  made  Tarot  Cards  to  
deceive the people to be directed in the wrong way and continue to 
live a cursed life. 
(5) God has given people Gifts through the Holy Spirit (Romans 12:6-
9)  to  help  people  in  there  Christian  walk  with  God.    Satan  has  
41

influenced  the  people  who  tell  Fortune  to  lie  and  tell  the  people  
they have gifts that come from God...but really their gifts are from 
Satan. 
 
• Romans 6:14-15  Sin must not be your Master
 ; for you do not live 
under  law  but  under  God's  grace.  What,  then?  Shall  we  sin,  
because  we  are  not  under  law  but  under  God's  grace?  By  no  
means!    Surely  you  know  that  when  you  surrender  yourselves  as  
slaves  to  obey  someone,  you  are  in  fact  the  slaves  of  the  master  
you  obey---either  of  sin,  which  results  in  death,  or  of  obedience,  
which results in being put right with God.  
 
How do you get set free from this boss or master?  Listen to what 
Jesus said... 
 
• John 8:31-36  So Jesus said to those who believed in him, "If you 
obey my teaching, you are really my disciples;  you will know the 
truth, and the truth will set you free." "We are the descendants of 
Abraham,"  they  answered,  "and  we  have  never  been  anybody's  
slaves.  What  do  you  mean,  then,  by  saying,  'You  will  be  free'?"  
Jesus said to them, "I am telling you the truth: everyone who sins 
is a slave of sin.  A slave does not belong to a family permanently, 
but a son belongs there forever.  If the Son sets you free, then you 
will be really free.

obey my teaching, you are really my disciples;  you will know the 
truth, and the truth will set you free." "We are the descendants of 
Abraham,"  they  answered,  "and  we  have  never  been  anybody's  
slaves.  What  do  you  mean,  then,  by  saying,  'You  will  be  free'?"  
Jesus said to them, "I am telling you the truth: everyone who sins 
is a slave of sin.  A slave does not belong to a family permanently, 
but a son belongs there forever.  If the Son sets you free, then you 
will be really free.  
 

43 
 
Surrender your life to Jesus and allow Him to set you free from 
your master of sin and Satan.  God gave us his laws so that we 
could  be  set  free  from  the  bondages  of  sin  and  this  world.    If  
you  really  believe  in  Jesus  like  you  say  you  do,  then  you  will  
obey him.  When you obey him, you will be set free...and when 
Jesus  sets  you  free,  you  will  be  free  indeed.    Your  boss  and  
master will then be Jesus Christ, the living son of God.

44 
 
Chapter 8 
Fear, Lies and Curses. 
 
In fortune telling, the whole objective is to lie to people.  One way 
fortune  teller’s  lie  is  by  saying  that  the  customer  has  curses  or  
some  negative  energy  in  their  life.    They  do  this  to  get  them  
afraid.  Fortune Tellers literally scare people into relying on them.   
Listen to what the Bible says:  
• Psams 63:11  Because God gives him victory, the king will rejoice. 
Those  who  make  promises  in  God's  name  will  praise  him,  but  the  
mouths of liars will be shut.  
• Proverbs  12:22    The  LORD  hates  liars,  but  is  pleased  with  those  
who keep their word.  
• Revelation   21:8      But   for   the   fearful,   and   unbelieving,   and   
abominable,  and  murderers,  and  fornicators,  and  sorcerers,  and  
idolaters, and all liars, their part shall be in the lake that burneth 
with fire and brimstone; which is the second death.  
• Proverbs  6:16-19    There  are  six  things  which  Jehovah  hateth;  
Yea, seven which are an abomination unto him:
 Haughty eyes, a 
lying  tongue,  And  hands  that  shed  innocent  blood;  A  heart  that  
deviseth  wicked  purposes,  Feet  that  are  swift  in  running  to  
mischief,  A  false  witness  that  uttereth  lies,  And  he  that  soweth  
discord among brethren.

All  of  these  things  are  done  in  fortune  telling  and  there  all  
abominations!!! 
 
Usually  the  lies  consist  of  telling  the  customer  that  they  have  
negative energy or they have a curse upon their life, but really the 
curse is on the fortune teller.   
Read what the Bible says about this... 
• John 8:44  You are the children of your father, the Devil, and you 
want  to  follow  your  father's  desires.  From  the  very  beginning  he  
was  a  murderer  and  has  never  been  on  the  side  of  truth,  because  
there is no truth in him. When he tells a lie, he is only doing what 
is natural to him, because he is a liar and the father of all lies.  
• 2  Peter  2:14-15    They  want  to  look  for  nothing  but  the  chance  to  
commit  adultery;  their  appetite  for  sin  is  never  satisfied.  They  
lead  weak  people  into  a  trap.  Their  hearts  are  trained  to  be  
greedy.  They  are  under  God's  curse!  They  have  left  the  straight  
path and have lost their way; they have followed the path taken by 
Balaam son of Beor, who loved the money he would get for doing 
wrong  
• 1 Corinthians 16:22  Whoever does not love the Lord---a curse on 
him! Marana tha---Our Lord, come! 
• John  14:23-24    Jesus  answered  him,  "Those  who  love  me  will  
obey my teaching. My Father will love them, and my Father and I 
will  come  to  them  and  live  with  them.  Those  who  do  not  love  me  
do not obey my teaching. And the teaching you have heard is not 
mine, but comes from the Father, who sent me.

• Proverbs  3:32-33    because  the  LORD  hates  people  who  do  evil,

righteous.

fortune  teller.    When  we  tell  the  customer  they  have  a  curse  on  
them and this strikes fear into their hearts, it can sometimes cause 
them make irrational decisions.  Listen to what the bible says about 
fear   
• Rom  8:14-15    Those  who  are  led  by  God's  Spirit  are  God's  
children. For the Spirit that God has given you does not make you 
slaves  and  cause  you  to  be  afraid;  instead,  the  Spirit  makes  you  
God's  children,  and  by  the  Spirit's  power  we  cry  out  to  God,  
"Father! my Father!"  
• Timothy 1:7  For God did not give us a spirit of fear, but of power 
and of love and of self-control.  Satan is the only one who tries to 
intimidate us and tries to strike fear in us.  Listen to what the bible 
says. 
• 1Peter  5:8    Be  alert,  be  on  watch!  Your  enemy,  the  devil  roams  
around like a roaring lion, looking for someone to devour. 
This  is  what  a  fortune  teller  does,  they  look  for  someone  who  is  
weak or unstable to strike fear into, much like a lion who seeks out 
his prey to devour it.  When we try to strike fear in people, we take 
on the character of Satan.

• 1John 4:18-21  There is no fear in love; perfect love drives out all 
fear.  So  then,  love  has  not  been  made  perfect  in  anyone  who  is  

47 
 
afraid, because fear has to do with punishment.  We love because 
God first loved us. If we say we love God, but hate others, we are 
liars.  For  we  cannot  love  God,  whom  we  have  not  seen,  if  we  do  
not  love  others,  whom  we  have  seen. The  command  that  Christ  
has given us is this: whoever loves God must love others also.  
 
If  we  say  we  love  God,  then  we  have  got  to  love  all  people.    We  
have  got  to  love  even  those  we  are  tempted  to  cheat  scam  and  
deceive.  We should love people and tell them the truth.   The way 
you search for people to tell fortune to, is the same way you should 
search  for  people  to  share  God’s  love  with...especially  if  you  
claim   to   be   a   Christian.      We   should   not   prey   on   other’s   
weaknesses,  but  try  to  help  them  overcome  their  weaknesses.    
God’s  love  casts  out  all  fears.    God  desires  to  cast  out  all  fears  
from people by His Love, and so when you prey on people’s fears, 
you are doing the opposite of God. 
 
Basically, what we have learned from this chapter is that when you 
lie  and  strike  fear  in  to  people,  you  are  following  the  ways  of  
Satan.    Also,  a  curse  will  be  on  your  house  as  well  as  theirs.    
Finally, if we do not repent, we will go to Hell! 
 
If  you  want  that  curse  lifted  from  you  and  your  families  listen  to  
what the Bible says

redeemed us from the curse that the Law brings; for the scripture

redeemed us from the curse that the Law brings; for the scripture 
47 
 
afraid, because fear has to do with punishment.  We love because 
God first loved us. If we say we love God, but hate others, we are 
liars.  For  we  cannot  love  God,  whom  we  have  not  seen,  if  we  do  
not  love  others,  whom  we  have  seen. The  command  that  Christ  
has given us is this: whoever loves God must love others also.  
 
If  we  say  we  love  God,  then  we  have  got  to  love  all  people.    We  
have  got  to  love  even  those  we  are  tempted  to  cheat  scam  and  
deceive.  We should love people and tell them the truth.   The way 
you search for people to tell fortune to, is the same way you should 
search  for  people  to  share  God’s  love  with...especially  if  you  
claim   to   be   a   Christian.      We   should   not   prey   on   other’s   
weaknesses,  but  try  to  help  them  overcome  their  weaknesses.    
God’s  love  casts  out  all  fears.    God  desires  to  cast  out  all  fears  
from people by His Love, and so when you prey on people’s fears, 
you are doing the opposite of God. 
 
Basically, what we have learned from this chapter is that when you 
lie  and  strike  fear  in  to  people,  you  are  following  the  ways  of  
Satan.    Also,  a  curse  will  be  on  your  house  as  well  as  theirs.    
Finally, if we do not repent, we will go to Hell! 
 
If  you  want  that  curse  lifted  from  you  and  your  families  listen  to  
what the Bible says

48 
 
says,  "Anyone  who  is  hanged  on  a  tree  is  under  God's  curse."    
Christ  did  this  in  order  that  the  blessing  which  God  promised  to  
Abraham might be given to the Gentiles by means of Christ Jesus, 
so that through faith we might receive the Spirit promised by God.  
Jesus took the curse upon himself, so when we abide and remain in 
Jesus then we will be set free from the curse... and from Hell. 
• John  15:4-7    Remain  united  to  me,  and  I  will  remain  united  to  
you.  A  branch  cannot  bear  fruit  by  itself;  it  can  do  so  only  if  it  
remains in the vine. In the same way you cannot bear fruit unless 
you remain in me. "I am the vine, and you are the branches. Those 
who remain in me, and I in them, will bear much fruit; for you can 
do  nothing  without  me. Those  who  do  not  remain  in  me  are  
thrown out like a branch and dry up; such branches are gathered 
up and thrown into the fire, where they are burned.  If you remain 
in me and my words remain in you, then you will ask for anything 
you wish, and you shall have it.  
 
Instead  of  abiding  in  lies,  curses  and  fear,  and  living  in  danger  of  
the  fires  of  Hell...Abide  in  Jesus,  so  that  you  will  live  a  life  of  
Truth,  blessings  and  Power.    (John  15:6)    You  receive  this  by  
remaining in God, remaining in his word, and being obedient.
