49 
 
Chapter 9 
Business is great (God is Blessing me) 
 
Many  people  believe  that  God  is  blessing  them  because  he  
sends  them  Customers,  and  they  make  money.    They  say  things  
like “God blessed me with an offica,” 
“God  blessed  me  with  a  good  Customer,”  or  “God  blessed  me  with  a  
large sum of  
Money,”  but  God  does  not  bless  disobedience  and  he  does  not  
bless those who break his  
laws.  
Let’s look at the Commandments; 
1. Exodus  20:3    "Worship  no  god  but  me.    The  People  start  
worshipping the fortuneteller. 
2. Exodus 20:5  Do not bow down to any idol or worship it, again, 
you  are  the  idol  the  customer  places  in  front  of  God.    Also,  there  
are images in the offica like; statues of Jesus, pictures, and crosses.  
These sometimes lead the costumer to believe in a certain “object” 
rather than God. 
3. Exodus   20:7      "Do   not   use   my   name   for   evil   purposes.      
Fortunetellers  will  use  the  name  of  God  in  their  business  for  
personal gain.   
4. Exodus  20:8    "Observe  the  Sabbath  and  keep  it  holy.  The  
customers  are  usually  not  permitted  to  go  to  church  unless  the  
fortune teller takes them. 
49

50 
 
5. Exodus  20:12    "Respect  your  father  and  your  mother.   The 
customer is told not to listen to their family members, and then told 
to keep their visits with them a secret. 
6. Exodus  20:13    "Do  not  commit  murder.
      Jesus  said  that  when  
you  have  hate  in  your  heart  for  a  person,  you  are  considered  a  
murderer.    Usually  the  Customer  begins  to  hate  certain  people  
because  the  fortuneteller  will  tell  things  like  “they  mean  bad  for  
you” or they are “Negative” for you. 
7. Exodus  20:14    "Do  not  commit  adultery.        
In  some  cases  
costumers are told to leave there spouses.  The customer then gets 
remarried  and  has  committed  adultery.    The  Bible  says  the  only  
way you will be permitted to leave your spouse is if they cheat on 
you.    In  other  cases,  the  customers  are  told  to  cheat  on  their  
spouses.  But even if there is no physical adultery being done, there 
is  definitely  Spiritual  adultery  being  done.    The  Bible  says  in  
Hosea  4:12    My  people  inquire  of  a  piece  of  wood,  and  their  
walking staff gives them oracles. For a spirit of whoredom has led 
them  astray,  and  they  have  left  their  God  to  play  the  
whore.  
(Spiritual adultery) 
8. Exodus  20:15    "Do  not  steal.      There  is  not  much  I  need  to  say  
about this commandment, because it is obvious that tactics fortune 
tellers employ; they steal people’s money by deception. 
9. Exodus  20:16  Do  not  accuse  anyone  falsely.    Sometimes  the  
customer  starts  accusing  people  of  things  they  are  not  guilty  of  
simply because they feel the fortune teller is right about the people 
in their life. 
50

10. Exodus 20:17  Thou shalt not covet.  The fortuneteller covets the 
customers money, jewelry, and other things they might have....that 
is the whole purpose of the business 
You  might  say  “well,  I  do  not  break  all  of  the  Commandments,”  
but  the  bible  says  in  James  2:10  that  whoever  breaks  one  
commandment  is  guilty  of  breaking  them  all.    You  might  even  
say, “Well then everyone is going to hell,” but Read this; 
• Hebrews 10:26  For there is no longer any sacrifice that will take 
away  sins  if  we  purposely  go  on  sinning  after  the  truth  has  been  
made known to us.  
There  is  a  big  difference  between  tripping  and  making  a  mistake,  
and truly falling on purpose.    
How about these verses? 
• Leviticus 19:11  "Do not steal or Cheat or lie.  
• Deuteronomy 25:16  The LORD hates people who cheat. 
• 1Corinthians 6:9  Surely you know that the wicked will not possess 
God's  Kingdom.  Do  not  fool  yourselves;  people  who  are  immoral  
or who worship idols or are adulterers or homosexual perverts  
• 1Corinthians 6:10  or who steal or are greedy or are drunkards or 
who  slander  others  or  are  thieves---none  of  these  will  possess  
God's Kingdom.  
How  can  God  Bless  you  when  what  you  are  doing  is  against  His  
Word.  God cannot bless sin, and God cannot go against His own 
word!!! 
• Deuteronomy 30:16  If you obey the commands of the LORD your 
God, which I give you today, if you love him, obey him, and keep 
51

God, which I give you today, if you love him, obey him, and keep 
51 
 
10. Exodus 20:17  Thou shalt not covet.  The fortuneteller covets the 
customers money, jewelry, and other things they might have....that 
is the whole purpose of the business 
You  might  say  “well,  I  do  not  break  all  of  the  Commandments,”  
but  the  bible  says  in  James  2:10  that  whoever  breaks  one  
commandment  is  guilty  of  breaking  them  all.    You  might  even  
say, “Well then everyone is going to hell,” but Read this; 
• Hebrews 10:26  For there is no longer any sacrifice that will take 
away  sins  if  we  purposely  go  on  sinning  after  the  truth  has  been  
made known to us.  
There  is  a  big  difference  between  tripping  and  making  a  mistake,  
and truly falling on purpose.    
How about these verses? 
• Leviticus 19:11  "Do not steal or Cheat or lie.  
• Deuteronomy 25:16  The LORD hates people who cheat. 
• 1Corinthians 6:9  Surely you know that the wicked will not possess 
God's  Kingdom.  Do  not  fool  yourselves;  people  who  are  immoral  
or who worship idols
 or are adulterers or homosexual perverts  
• 1Corinthians 6:10  or who steal or are greedy or are drunkards or 
who  slander  others  or  are  thieves---none  of  these  will  possess  
God's Kingdom.  
How  can  God  Bless  you  when  what  you  are  doing  is  against  His  
Word.  God cannot bless sin, and God cannot go against His own 
word!!!

52 
 
all  his  laws,  then  you  will  prosper  and  become  a  nation  of  many

  you  in  the  land  that  you  
are about to occupy.  
When  you  Obey  God,  then  you  will  see  the  Blessings  of  the  
Lord!!! 
You might respond, “well who is blessing me, then?” 
Before Jesus started His Ministry He was tempted by the devil. 
• Mat 4:8-9  Then the Devil took Jesus to a very high mountain and 
showed  him  all  the  kingdoms  of  the  world  in  all  their  greatness. 
"All  this  I  will  give  you,"  the  Devil  said,  "if  you  kneel  down  and  
worship me."  
Jesus  was  tempted  by  the  devil,  but  He  did  not  give  in  to  
temptation.    The  devil  showed  Jesus  everything  in  the  World  and  
all  that  it  has  to  offer;  money,  fame,  power  and  even  authority.    
Jesus  only  needed  to  do  one  thing  and  it  would  all  be  his;  bow  
down and worship the devil.  One of the definitions for Worship is 
“to do whatever is pleasing.”  Living a life that pleases God is an 
act  of  Worship.    On  the  other  hand,  it  is  also  an  act  of  Worship  
when  you  please  the  devil  by  how  you  live  your  life.    When  you  
steal,  scam,  lie,  cheat,  and  take  people  from  the  True  God,  this  
pleases  the  devil.    When  you  tell  fortune,  you  are  worshiping  the  
devil, and to reward you, he gives you the things of the world! 
Jesus  said  it’s  Possible  for  you  to  gain  the  whole  world  and  still  
loose you soul! 
• Mat  16:24-26    Then  Jesus  told  his  disciples,  "If  anyone  would  
come  after  me,  let  him  deny  himself  and  take  up  his  cross  and  
52 
 
all  his  laws,  then  you  will  prosper  and  become  a  nation  of  many

  you  in  the  land  that  you  
are about to occupy.

come  after  me,  let  him  deny  himself  and  take  up  his  cross  and  

53 
 
follow  me.  For  whoever  would  save  his  life  will  lose  it,  but  
whoever  loses  his  life  for  my  sake  will  find  it.  For  what  will  it  
profit a man if he gains the whole world and forfeits his soul? Or 
what shall a man give in return for his soul?  
Money from fortune telling is not a Blessing from God, but it is a 
trap, and a snare of the devil to keep you doing his will.  His will is 
that you lie, cheat, steal and keep people from God, and when you 
do  this,  he  will  give  you  the  things  of  the  World  to  keep  you  
ensnared.    Have  you  ever  noticed  how  the  money  you  make  from  
fortune telling never lasts, why is this? 
• Proverbs  21:6    The  riches  you  get  by  dishonesty  soon  disappear,  
but not before they lead you into the jaws of death.  
• Proverbs  13:11    Money  wrongly  gotten  will  disappear  bit  by  bit;  
money earned little by little will grow and grow.  
• Proverbs 10:2  Wealth you get by dishonesty will do you no good, 
but honesty can save your life.  
Or maybe you are a person that has actually saved there money but 
is probably living in misery.  
• 1 Timothy 6:10  for the Love of Money a root of all kinds of evil: 
which some reaching after have been led astray from the faith, and 
have pierced themselves through with many sorrows.  (this is why 
you are miserable)  
• Proverbs  11:7    When  the  wicked  die,  their  hope  dies  with  them.  
Confidence placed in riches comes to nothing.  
 
Listen to what Jesus said about putting Money in front of God.

• Matthew  6:24    "You  cannot  be  a  slave  of  two  masters;  you  will  
hate  one  and  love  the  other;  you  will  be  loyal  to  one  and  despise  
the other. You cannot serve both God and money.  
Obey God!  Be Faithful to God!  Do something you can be honest 
in.    The  money  is  not  worth  going to hell over.  REPENT!  Do 
what  Jesus  did  Mathew  4:10    Then  Jesus  answered,  "Go  away,  
Satan!  The  scripture  says,  'Worship  the  Lord  your  God  and  serve  
only him!' "  
• Proverbs  22:4    Obey  the  LORD,  be  humble,  and  you  will  get  
riches, honor, and a long life.  
 
How do you repent of the Love of Money? 
• Revelation  3:17-19    You  say,  'I  am  rich  and  well  off;  I  have  all  I  
need.' But you do not know how miserable and pitiful you are! You 
are  poor,  naked,  and  blind.    I  advise  you,  then,  to  buy  gold  from  
me, pure gold, in order to be rich. Buy also white clothing to dress 
yourself  and  cover  up  your  shameful  nakedness.  Buy  also  some  
ointment  to  put  on  your  eyes,  so  that  you  may  see.  I  rebuke  and  
punish  all  whom  I  love.  Be  in  earnest,  then,  and  turn  from  your  
sins.    
Basically what Jesus is saying in this passage is “let me help you, 
because  you  cannot  help  yourself.”    Ask  for  His  help  right  now,  
and Jesus will help you.  Jesus will deliver you.  He will save you 
and  sustain  you.  Remember  he  is  Jehova-Jirah  the  Lord  our  
provider.

55 
 
Chapter 10 
God understands (God knows my heart) 
 
I  have  heard  fortune  tellers  say  things  like,  “God  understands  I  
have to do this because this is all I know,” or “God understands I 
have to do this, because I’m married to a man that is forcing me do 
it,”  or  even  “God  understands  because  of  my  culture,  I  am  a  
Gypsy. 
Listen to God’s own words concerning this matter... 
• Romans  1:20-22    Ever  since  God  created  the  world,  his  invisible  
qualities,  both  his  eternal  power  and  his  divine  nature,  have  been  
clearly  seen;  they  are  perceived  in  the  things  that  God  has  made.  
So  those  people  have  no  excuse  at  all!  They  know  God,  but  they  
do  not  give  him  the  honor  that  belongs  to  him,  nor  do  they  thank  
him. Instead, their thoughts have become complete nonsense, and 
their  empty  minds  are  filled  with  darkness.  They  say  they  are  
wise, but they are fools;  
• Jude  1:4-7    For  some  godless  people  have  slipped  in  unnoticed  
among  us,  persons  who  distort  the  message  about  the  grace  of  
our  God  in  order  to  excuse  their  immoral  ways,  and  who  reject  
Jesus  Christ,  our  only  Master  and  Lord.  Long  ago  the  Scriptures  
predicted  the  condemnation  they  have  received.  For  even  though  
you  know  all  this,  I  want  to  remind  you  of  how  the  Lord  once  
rescued  the  people  of  Israel  from  Egypt,  but  afterward  destroyed  
those who did not believe.  Remember the angels who did not stay 
within  the  limits  of  their  proper  authority,  but  abandoned  their  
55 
 
Chapter 10 
God understands (God knows my heart) 
 
I  have  heard  fortune  tellers  say  things  like,  “God  understands  I  
have to do this because this is all I know,” or “God understands I 
have to do this, because I’m married to a man that is forcing me do 
it,”  or  even  “God  understands  because  of  my  culture,  I  am  a  
Gypsy. 
Listen to God’s own words concerning this matter... 
• Romans  1:20-22    Ever  since  God  created  the  world,  his  invisible  
qualities,  both  his  eternal  power  and  his  divine  nature,  have  been  
clearly  seen;  they  are  perceived  in  the  things  that  God  has  made.  
So  those  people  have  no  excuse  at  all!  They  know  God,  but  they  
do  not  give  him  the  honor  that  belongs  to  him,  nor  do  they  thank  
him. Instead, their thoughts have become complete nonsense, and

wise, but they are fools;

56 
 
own  dwelling  place:  they  are  bound  with  eternal  chains  in  the  
darkness below, where God is keeping them for that great Day on 
which they will be condemned. Remember Sodom and Gomorrah, 
and the nearby towns, whose people acted as those angels did and 
indulged  in  sexual  immorality  and  perversion:  they  suffer  the  
punishment of eternal fire as a plain warning to all.  
God did not spare the Angels when they rebelled against Him, but 
they were cast out of Heaven and into Hell.  God did not spare the 
people  of  Sodom  and  Gomorrah  when  they  rebelled  against  Him;  
God  sent  fire  from  the  sky  to  destroy  them.    God  destroys  and  
judges  wickedness.      Listen  to  what  the  Bible  says  about  people  
who are warned to stop what they are doing but continue in it any 
way because they feel Gods grace will cover them. 
• Hebrews  10:26-31    For  there  is  no  longer  any  sacrifice  that  will  
take  away  sins  if  we  purposely  go  on  sinning  after  the  truth  has  
been made known to us. Instead, all that is left is to wait in fear for 
the  coming  Judgment  and  the  fierce  fire  which  will  destroy  those  
who oppose God!   Anyone who disobeys the Law of Moses is put 
to  death  without  any  mercy  when  judged  guilty  from  the  evidence  
of two or more witnesses. What, then, of those who despise the Son 
of  God?  who treat as a cheap thing the  blood  of  God's  covenant  
which purified them from sin? who insult the Spirit of grace? Just 
think how much worse is the punishment they will deserve! For we 
know  who  said,  "I  will  take  revenge,  I  will  repay";  and  who  also  
said,  "The  Lord  will  judge  his  people."    It  is  a  terrifying  thing  to  
fall into the hands of the living God!

Jesus gave us a Parable in the Bible to illustrate just how we have 
all  been  called  to  serve  God  and  enter  into  the  great  feast  in  
heaven. 
• Luke 14:16-20  Jesus said to him, "There was once a man who was 
giving a great feast to which he invited many people.  When it was 
time  for  the  feast,  he  sent  his  servant  to  tell  his  guests,  'Come,  
everything  is  ready!'    But  they  all  began,  one  after  another,  to  
make Excuses. The first one told the servant, 'I have bought a field 
and must go and look at it; please accept my apologies.'   Another 
one said, 'I have bought five pairs of oxen and am on my way to try 
them  out;  please  accept  my  apologies.'    Another  one  said,  'I  have  
just gotten married, and for that reason I cannot come.'  
Have  you  been  putting  God  off  and  making  excuses?  Listen  to  
what Jesus said about those servants that made excuses... 
• Luke 14:24  I tell you all that none of those who were invited 
will 
taste my dinner!' "  
You will not enter in to the great Feast of God!!! 
 
What  should  a  fortuneteller  do?    Stop  making  Excuses,  repent  of  
your  ways,  and  then  give  it  up.    God  will  provide.    Jesus  says  in  
Revelation... 
• Revelation  3:20-21    Listen!  I  stand  at  the  door  and  knock;  if  any  
hear my voice and open the door, I will come into their house and 
eat  with  them,  and  they  will  eat  with  me.  To  those  who  win  the

59 
 
Chapter 11 
Still NO Peace 
Have  you  ever  noticed  that  no  matter  how  much  money  you  have  
or  what  you  buy,  you’re  still  miserable,  depressed,  worried,  and  
lonely  and  you  have  no  Peace?    My  wife  Gina  and  I  had  a  
Corvette,  a  Cadillac  Escalade,  and  a  new  Honda  Motorcycle,  2  
Rolexes,  Jewelry,  Big  screen  TVs,  Big  Houses  and  $50,000  put  
away  in  the  Bank...all  from  telling  fortunes  and  scamming.    We  
lived  a  life  of  luxury,  but  I  was  doing  despicable  things  that  I  am  
embarrassed to mention.  Gina and I even tried to commit suicide 
on  a  few  different  occasions  because  of  our  misery.    Even  though  
we had “stuff,” we had no Peace in our lives. The Money and the 
Possessions  did  not  and  cannot  make  anyone  happy.    In  front  of  
people we wore our fake masks of happiness, but behind the scenes 
we  were  angry,  hurt,  depressed  and  frustrated.    You  might  be  
thinking that if you can just get more “stuff” then you will be truly 
happy, but take it from me, one who had all the “stuff”...it   never 
brings Peace.  Those who live in willful sin and disobey God will 
never have true peace, and you will never be satisfied.  Things that 
feed  the  desires  of  the  flesh  just  cause  the  flesh  to  desire  more  
things...and  the  cycle  never  stops...and  there  is  never  enough  
“things.” 
 
Let’s read what the Bible says about this.  God was talking to the 
Children  of  Israel  and  telling  them  what  would  happen  if  they  
disobey.  (If you can read all of Deuteronomy chapter 28)

brings Peace.  Those who live in willful sin and disobey God will 
never have true peace, and you will never be satisfied.  Things that 
feed  the  desires  of  the  flesh  just  cause  the  flesh  to  desire  more  
things...and  the  cycle  never  stops...and  there  is  never  enough  
“things.” 
 
Let’s read what the Bible says about this.  God was talking to the 
Children  of  Israel  and  telling  them  what  would  happen  if  they  
disobey.  (If you can read all of Deuteronomy chapter 28)  
59 
 
Chapter 11 
Still NO Peace 
Have  you  ever  noticed  that  no  matter  how  much  money  you  have  
or  what  you  buy,  you’re  still  miserable,  depressed,  worried,  and  
lonely  and  you  have  no  Peace?    My  wife  Gina  and  I  had  a  
Corvette,  a  Cadillac  Escalade,  and  a  new  Honda  Motorcycle,  2  
Rolexes,  Jewelry,  Big  screen  TVs,  Big  Houses  and  $50,000  put  
away  in  the  Bank...all  from  telling  fortunes  and  scamming.    We  
lived  a  life  of  luxury,  but  I  was  doing  despicable  things  that  I  am  
embarrassed to mention.  Gina and I even tried to commit suicide 
on  a  few  different  occasions  because  of  our  misery.    Even  though  
we had “stuff,” we had no Peace in our lives. The Money and the 
Possessions  did  not  and  cannot  make  anyone  happy.    In  front  of  
people we wore our fake masks of happiness, but behind the scenes 
we  were  angry,  hurt,  depressed  and  frustrated.    You  might  be  
thinking that if you can just get more “stuff” then you will be truly 
happy, but take it from me, one who had all the “stuff”...it   never 
brings Peace.  Those who live in willful sin and disobey God will 
never have true peace, and you will never be satisfied.  Things that 
feed  the  desires  of  the  flesh  just  cause  the  flesh  to  desire  more  
things...and  the  cycle  never  stops...and  there  is  never  enough  
“things.” 
 
Let’s read what the Bible says about this.  God was talking to the 
Children  of  Israel  and  telling  them  what  would  happen  if  they  
disobey.  (If you can read all of Deuteronomy chapter 28)

• Deuteronomy  28:65-67  You  will  find  no  peace
  anywhere,  no  
place  to  call  your  own;  the  LORD  will  overwhelm  you  with  
anxiety,  hopelessness,  and  despair.  Your  life  will  always  be  in  
danger. Day and night you will be filled  with  terror,  and  you  will  
live in constant fear of death.  Your hearts will pound with fear at 
everything you see. Every morning you will wish for evening; every 
evening you will wish for morning.  
Do  you  feel  like  this?  It  is  because  of  the  sin  that  is  in  your  life,  
and  not  only  fortune  telling  will  make  your  life  like  this,  but  any  
sin  that  you  are  in  bondage  to.    Any  sin  will  bring  all  these  evils  
upon you.  If you do not repent, you will never have true peace and 
happiness.    To  put  it  bluntly,  if  you  are  a  sinner,  then  you  are  in  
danger  of  Hell.    Read  the  following  verse  and  examine  your  
life....

am telling you again: No one who does these things will share in 
the blessings of God's kingdom.  
You may have to give up a lot more than just fortune telling, and I 
can  guarantee  if  you  tell  fortune  there  are  a  lot  of  other  things  in  
your  life  as  well.    I  myself  was  caught  up  in  every  sin  you  can  
imagine;  adultery,  fortune  telling,  cursing,  gossiping,  fighting,  
anger,  lust,  unforgiveness,  lying,  drunkenness  and  idolatry.    I  do  
not say those things to brag, but to say; if God can forgive me and 
can use me after all that, he can use you too.  
 
How did so much sin creep into my life? 
• James  1:13-16  If  we  are  tempted  by  such  trials,  we  must  not  say,  
"This temptation comes from God." For God cannot be tempted by 
evil,  and  he  himself  tempts  no  one.  But  we  are  tempted  when  we  
are drawn away and trapped by our own evil desires. Then our evil 
desires  conceive  and  give  birth  to  sin;  and  sin,  when  it  is  full-
grown, gives birth to death.  Do not be deceived, my dear friends!  
The  more  I  sinned,  the  bigger  the  hunger  grew  inside  of  me  for  
more sin.   I began to allow things in my life that I never thought I 
would  indulge  in.    Sin  gives  birth  to  more  sin,  and  if  we  do  not  
stop sinning and repent... there will be death.  Stop being deceived 
by  Satan,  and  be  delivered  and  set  free  from  the  bondage  of  your  
sins.  The Only way to do this is to Listen and Obey the Prince of 
Peace, which is Jesus Christ!

• Romans  14:17-19    For  God's  Kingdom  is  not  a  matter  of  eating  
and  drinking,  but  of  the  righteousness,  peace,  and  joy  which  the

another.  
• Phillipians 4:7-9    Then,  because  you  belong  to  Christ  Jesus,  God 
will bless you with peace that no one can completely understand. 
And this peace will control the way you think and feel. Finally, my 
friends, keep  your  minds  on  whatever  is  true,  pure,  right,  holy,  
friendly, and proper. Don't ever stop thinking about what is truly 
worthwhile  and  worthy  of  praise.  You  know  the  teachings  I  gave  
you,  and  you  know  what  you  heard  me  say  and  saw  me  do.  So 
follow my example. And God, who gives peace, will be with you.  
 
As  Paul  said  in  the  Bible,  I  say  to  you;  follow  my  example  and  
repent.    Turn  from  fortune  telling.    Gina  and  I,  along  with  our  
children, are living a far more blessed life now than we ever have, 
and it is all because we are following Jehovah Shalom. (The Lord 
our  Peace)  You  can  call  upon  The  Prince  of  Peace  right  now,  
and  ask  Him  to  deliver  you  from  your  sin.    He  desires  to  give  
you true Peace and happiness.   Do you desire to have it?

63 
 
Chapter 12 
What about the other Businesses. 
 
Some fortune tellers might try to justify their actions by turning the 
spot light on someone else’s action.  They may say something like, 
“Well what about the other businesses that are wrong?  Aren’t they 
just as bad?”   
• Philippians  2:12    So  then,  my  beloved,  even  as  ye  have  always  
obeyed,  not  as  in  my  presence  only,  but  now  much  more  in  my  
absence, work out your own salvation with fear and trembling;  
You  need  to  worry  about  your  own  salvation,  and  then  you  can  
begin to worry about other people’s lives.  In God’s eyes, sin is sin.  
Their  businesses  are  wrong  also,  but  you  do  not  measure  yourself  
by  man,  but  by  God’s  word.    You  will  not  stand  in  judgment  for  
what  others  do,  or  even  how  you  compare  to  others,  but  you  will  
stand in judgment for every deed you commit.   
 
But since we are on the subject, we might as well expose their sins 
too, because we do not desire that anyone should perish and go to 
Hell, but that all are saved.

evil they do. They cheat one another; they break in to Houses
 and 
63 
 
Chapter 12 
What about the other Businesses. 
 
Some fortune tellers might try to justify their actions by turning the 
spot light on someone else’s action.  They may say something like, 
“Well what about the other businesses that are wrong?  Aren’t they 
just as bad?”

• Matthew  22:38-40  This  is  the  first  and  great  commandment.  
 
And 
the second is like it: ‘You shall love your neighbor as yourself.’
 
On 
these two commandments hang all the Law and the Prophets.” 
• James  5:4  Look!  The  wages  you  failed  to  pay  the  workmen  who  
mowed  your  fields  are  crying  out  against  you.  The  cries  of  the  
harvesters have reached the ears of the Lord Almighty. 
5
You have 
lived  on  earth  in  luxury  and  self-indulgence.  You  have  fattened  
yourselves  in  the  day  of  slaughter.  
6
You  have  condemned  and  
murdered innocent men, who were not opposing you. 
 
In  all  of  these  so-called  “businesses,”  people  think  they  are  just  
earning money, but they are earning God’s wrath 
• Micah  6:10-11    In  the  houses  of  evil  people  are  treasures  which  
they got dishonestly. They use false measures, a thing that I hate. 
How can I forgive those who use false scales and weights?  
• Ephesians 4:27-30  Don't give the Devil a chance. If you used to 
rob, you must stop robbing and start working, in order to earn an 
honest living for yourself and to be able to help the poor.   Do not 
use  harmful  words,  but  only  helpful  words,  the  kind  that  build  up  
and  provide  what  is  needed,  so  that  what  you  say  will  do  good  to  
those  who  hear  you.    And  do  not  make  God's  Holy  Spirit  sad;  for  
the Spirit is God's mark of ownership on you, a guarantee that the 
Day will come when God will set you free.

• 1Corinthians 6:10  or who steal or are greedy or are drunkards or 
who  slander  others  or  are  thieves---none  of  these  will  possess  
God's Kingdom.  
 
Fortune Telling 
• Leviticus  20:6-7    "I  will  condemn  people  who  turn  to  mediums  
and   psychics   and   chase   after   them   as   though   they   were   
prostitutes. I will exclude them from the people. "Live holy lives. 
Be holy because I am the LORD your God. Obey my laws, and live 
by them. I am the LORD who sets you apart as holy.  
 
All  these  businesses  are  sin,  but  Fortune  telling  literally  leads  
people away from the truth.   In these other businesses, the people 
who  are  involved  do  sin,  but  those  who  tell  fotune  are  literally  
leading  others  away  from  God  and  into  sin.    Jesus  said  this  about  
people who lead others from the truth.  
• Matthew 18:6-7  "If anyone should cause one of these little ones to 
lose  his  faith  in  me,  it  would  be  better  for  that  person  to  have  a  
large millstone tied around his neck and be drowned in the deep 
sea. How  terrible  for  the  world  that  there  are  things  that  make  
people  lose  their  faith!  Such  things  will  always  happen---but  how  
terrible for the one who causes them!  
WOW!  The person should be drowned in the sea with a 2000 
pound rock tied to his neck, it is better for you to hurt yourself, 
before you hurt one of God’s children.

67 
 
The  Bible  says  that  all  people  who  cause  others  to  sin  will  be  
thrown into Hell 
• Matthew  13:41-42    the  Son  of  Man  will  send  out  his  angels  to  
gather  up  out  of  his  Kingdom  all  those  who  cause  people  to  sin  
and  all  others  who  do  evil  things,  and  they  will  throw  them  into  
the fiery furnace, where they will cry and gnash their teeth.  
• 1John 2:9-11  If we say that we are in the light, yet hate others, we 
are in the darkness to this very hour.  If we love others, we live in 
the  light,  and  so  there  is  nothing  in  us  that  will  cause  someone  
else to sin.   But if we hate others, we are in the darkness; we walk 
in it and do not know where we are going, because the darkness 
has made us blind.  
 
Do  not  justify  your  own  sin  by  pointing  the  finger  at  others  and  
accusing  them.    Admit  your  sins.    Confess  your  sins.    Choose  to  
walk  in  the  light.    Open  your  eyes,  because  this  is  serious.  Sin  is  
darkness, and if you stay in sin you will end up in eternal darkness.  
What should you do? Do what Jesus said to do 
• Mat 18:8-9  "If your hand or your foot makes you lose your faith, 
cut it off and throw it away! It is better for you to enter life without 
a  hand  or  a  foot  than  to  keep  both  hands  and  both  feet  and  be  
thrown  into  the  eternal  fire.  And  if  your  eye  makes  you  lose  your  
faith, take it out and throw it away! It is better for you to enter life 
with  only  one  eye  than  to  keep  both  eyes  and  be  thrown  into  the  
fire of hell.  
67 
 
The  Bible  says  that  all  people  who  cause  others  to  sin  will  be  
thrown into Hell 
• Matthew  13:41-42    the  Son  of  Man  will  send  out  his  angels  to  
gather  up  out  of  his  Kingdom  all  those  who  cause  people  to  sin  
and  all  others  who  do  evil  things,  and  they  will  throw  them  into  
the fiery furnace, where they will cry and gnash their teeth.  
• 1John 2:9-11  If we say that we are in the light, yet hate others, we 
are in the darkness to this very hour.  If we love others, we live in 
the  light,  and  so  there  is  nothing  in  us  that  will  cause  someone  
else to sin.   But if we hate others, we are in the darkness; we walk 
in it and do not know where we are going, because the darkness 
has made us blind.  
 
Do  not  justify  your  own  sin  by  pointing  the  finger  at  others  and  
accusing  them.    Admit  your  sins.    Confess  your  sins.    Choose  to  
walk  in  the  light.    Open  your  eyes,  because  this  is  serious.  Sin  is  
darkness, and if you stay in sin you will end up in eternal darkness.

68 
 
Is Jesus saying literally pluck your eye out and cut your hand off?  
No,  of  course  not,  but  He  is  giving  you  an  illustration.    Basically  
what he is saying is; whatever is in your life that is causing you to 
sin, get it out of your life.  It is better for you to get it out of your 
life and go to heaven, then to keep it in your life and go to hell.  It 
is time to work out your salvation with fear and trembling.  Amen.

Chapter 13 
Consequences Clarified. 
 
Please read the entire book before you read this chapter so you will 
fully  understand  why  I  feel  the  need  to  be  so  straightforward.    
These  are  not  my  words;  these  are  the  words  of  the  Living  God.    
This chapter is to sum up the consequences of the sin of the fortune 
teller... or anyone for that matter, who uses deception to lie, steal, 
cheat and scam or live their life in-bondage to sin. 
• Galatians 5:19-21  People's desires make them give in to immoral 
ways,  filthy  thoughts,  and  shameful  deeds.  They  worship  idols,  
practice  witchcraft,  hate  others,  and  are  hard  to  get  along  with.  
People  become  jealous,  angry,  and  selfish.  They  not  only  argue  
and cause trouble, but they are envious. They get drunk, carry on 
at wild parties, and do other evil things as well. I told you before, 
and  I  am  telling  you  again:  No  one  who  does  these  things  will  
share in the blessings of God's kingdom.  
• Deuteronomy  18:10-12    You  must  never  sacrifice  your  sons  or  
daughters  by  burning  them  alive,  practice  black  magic,  be  a  
fortuneteller,  witch,  or  sorcerer, cast  spells,  ask  ghosts  or  spirits  
for  help,  or  consult  the  dead.  Whoever  does  these  things  is  
disgusting  to  the  LORD.  The  LORD  your  God  is  forcing  these  
nations out of your way because of their disgusting practices.  
• Ephesians 5:3-5  You are God's people, so don't let it be said that 
any  of  you  are  immoral  or  indecent  or  greedy.  Don't  use  dirty  or  
69

foolish  or  filthy  words.  Instead,  say  how  thankful  you  are.  Being 
greedy,  indecent,  or  immoral  is  just  another  way  of  worshiping  
idols.  You  can  be  sure  that  people  who  behave  in  this  way  will  
never be part of the kingdom that belongs to Christ and to God.  
• Revelation  22:15    But  outside  the  city  are  the  perverts  and  those  
who practice  magic,  the  immoral  and  the  murderers,  those  who

"Every man or woman who is a medium or a psychic must be put 
to  death.  They  must  be  stoned  to  death  because  they  deserve  to  
die."  
• 2Peter 2:4-9  God did not spare the angels who sinned, but threw 
them into hell, where they are kept chained in darkness, waiting 
for  the  Day  of  Judgment.    God  did  not  spare  the  ancient  world,  
but brought the flood on the world of godless people; the only ones 
he saved were Noah, who preached righteousness, and seven other 
people.    God  condemned  the  cities  of  Sodom  and  Gomorrah,  
destroying  them  with  fire,  and  made  them  an  example  of  what  
will happen to the godless.  He rescued Lot, a good man, who was 
distressed  by  the  immoral  conduct  of  lawless  people.  That  good  
man lived among them, and day after day he suffered agony as he 
saw  and  heard  their  evil  actions.  And  so  the  Lord  knows  how  to  
rescue godly people from their trials and how to keep the wicked 
under punishment for the Day of Judgment,

• 1Co 6:9-12  Don't you know that evil people won't have a share 
in  the  blessings  of  God's  kingdom?  Don't  fool  yourselves!  No  
one who is immoral or worships idols or is unfaithful in marriage 
or  is  a  pervert  or  behaves  like  a  homosexual  will  share  in  God's  
kingdom.  Neither  will  any  thief or  greedy  person  or  drunkard  or  
anyone who curses and cheats others. Some of you used to be like 
that. But now the name of our Lord Jesus Christ and the power of 
God's  Spirit  have  washed  you  and  made  you  holy  and  acceptable  
to God.  Some of you say, "We can do anything we want to." But I 
tell  you  that  not  everything  is  good  for  us.  So  I  refuse  to  let  
anything have power over me.  
 
Because of these consequences, take Gods advice... 
• James  1:13-17    Let  no  one  say  when  he  is  tempted,  "I  am  being  
tempted  by  God,"  for  God  cannot  be  tempted  with  evil,  and  he  
himself tempts no one. But each person is tempted when he is lured 
and  enticed  by  his  own  desire.  Then  desire  when  it  has  conceived  
gives birth to sin, and sin when it is fully grown brings forth death. 
Do  not  be  deceived,  my  beloved  brothers.  Therefore  let  anyone  
who thinks that he stands take heed lest he fall.  
• 1Corinthians  10:13-14    No  temptation  has  overtaken  you  that  is  
not  common  to  man.  God  is  faithful,  and  he  will  not  let  you  be  
tempted beyond your ability, but with the temptation he will also 
provide the way of escape, that you may be able to endure it.  
• Therefore, my beloved, flee from idolatry.  
71

• Therefore, my beloved, flee from idolatry.

• 1Co 6:9-12  Don't you know that evil people won't have a share 
in  the  blessings  of  God's  kingdom?  Don't  fool  yourselves!  No  
one who is immoral or worships idols or is unfaithful in marriage 
or  is  a  pervert  or  behaves  like  a  homosexual  will  share  in  God's  
kingdom.  Neither  will  any  thief or  greedy  person  or  drunkard  or  
anyone who curses and cheats others. Some of you used to be like 
that. But now the name of our Lord Jesus Christ and the power of 
God's  Spirit  have  washed  you  and  made  you  holy  and  acceptable  
to God.  Some of you say, "We can do anything we want to." But I 
tell  you  that  not  everything  is  good  for  us.  So  I  refuse  to  let  
anything have power over me.  
 
Because of these consequences, take Gods advice... 
• James  1:13-17    Let  no  one  say  when  he  is  tempted,  "I  am  being  
tempted  by  God,"  for  God  cannot  be  tempted  with  evil,  and  he  
himself tempts no one. But each person is tempted when he is lured 
and  enticed  by  his  own  desire.  Then  desire  when  it  has  conceived  
gives birth to sin, and sin when it is fully grown brings forth death. 
Do  not  be  deceived,  my  beloved  brothers.  Therefore  let  anyone  
who thinks that he stands take heed lest he fall.  
• 1Corinthians  10:13-14    No  temptation  has  overtaken  you  that  is  
not  common  to  man.  God  is  faithful,  and  he  will  not  let  you  be  
tempted beyond your ability, but with the temptation he will also 
provide the way of escape, that you may be able to endure it.  
• Therefore, my beloved, flee from idolatry.

• Ephesians 4:27-28  Don't give the Devil a chance. If you used to 
rob, you must stop robbing and start working
, in order to earn an 
honest living for yourself and to be able to help the poor.  
• 1Peter  3:10-12    As  the  scripture  says,  "If  you  want  to  enjoy  life  
and wish to see good times, you must keep from speaking evil and 
stop telling lies. You must turn away from evil and do good; you 
must  strive  for  peace  with  all  your  heart.    For  the  Lord  watches  
over  the  righteous  and  listens  to  their  prayers;  but  he  opposes  
those who do evil. 
• Matthew 4:17  From that time began Jesus to preach, and to say, 
Repent ye; for the kingdom of heaven is at hand.  
 
And  just  like  the  customers  do  not  know  when  the  fortune  teller  
will strike and rob them...God is coming back in that same way. 
• Revelation  3:3  Remember,  then,  what  you  were  taught  and  what  
you  heard;  obey  it  and  turn  from  your  sins.  If  you  do  not  wake  
up, I will come upon you like a thief, and you will not even know 
the time when I will come.  
 
Let us repent and be a true follower of Jesus Christ.  God died 
for you to give you life.  Read the next chapter to learn how to 
submit and give your life to Jesus

73 
 
Chapter 14 
Renouncing & Repenting 
 
Definition of Repent: To feel pain, sorrow or regret for something 
done or spoken; as, to repent that we have injured or wounded the 
feelings of a friend. A person repents only of what he himself has 
done or said.  Repent from past, change of heart. 
 
Repenting  means  that  you  realize  you  have  done  wrong.    You  
literally  feel  a  hurt  and  a  sorrow,  because  you  realize  the  trouble  
you  have  caused,  so  you  turn  your  back  on  whatever  it  was  you  
were doing.  At this point you decide in your heart that you do not 
want to do it anymore!  This is how we need to repent from fortune 
telling or any other sins in our lives.  We must stop completely and 
refuse to look back.  False repentance is like the husband and wife 
who were arguing about a direction.  The husband said it was this 
way  and  the  wife  said  the  other.    The  husband  decided  to  go  his  
way,  and  soon  found  out  that  he  was  going  the  wrong  direction.  
He said he was sorry, he confessed his wrong, but he did not repent 
because he did not turn around.  It is not until we turn around that 
we truly repent.  Gina and I repented of our evil ways and gave up 
the sins that were destroying us.  We did this because the Word of 
God  was  revealed  to  us  in  such  a  way  that  we  found  out  how  to  
become  True  Christians,  and  what  God  desired  from  us.  Ever  
since, we have been more than blessed.  We may not have as much 
“stuff” as we use to have, but we have greater treasures; joy, love, 
73

since, we have been more than blessed.  We may not have as much 
“stuff” as we use to have, but we have greater treasures; joy, love, 

74 
 
purpose  fulfillment,  and  peace.    God  always  supplies  our  needs.    
We  have  five  kids;  Savannah,  David,  Noel,  Gia  and  Joy,  and  the  
Bible  says  if  I  obey,  my  children  will  be  blessed.    My  Family  is  
blessed!  
 
How do you make this same change? 
   
Well  first  you  need  to  understand  that  it  will  not  be  easy,  but  the  
Bible  says  we  can  do  all  things  through  Christ  who  gives  us  
strength.    I  remember  the  day  I  went to Gina and told her that we 
needed  to  give  up  fortune  telling.    She  responded,  “I  can’t...  I’m  
not  ready  yet”.      I  went  to  the  Lord  in  prayer  and  prayed,  “God,  
you  never  forced  me  to  do  anything,  so  I  am  not  going  to  force  
Gina either.”  I continued to pray, Gina and I went to church every 
Sunday, and I was patiently waiting on God to speak to her.  About 
a month went by, and she told me she was ready to give it up, and 
we did.  The Key factors to success is; constant Prayer, reading the 
word every day, and Continuing in Church.  No one can force you 
to quit, the Choice is yours.  God did not make robots, but he made 
people with free will whom he hopes will decide to serve him out 
of  their  love  for  him.      And  don’t  let  fear  stand  in  the  way,  God  
will provide for you.  One of God’s names in the Bible is Jehovah 
Jireh, and it means “The Lord my Provider!” 
 
The Following are some of the things you need to do in Order for 
you to see God Move in your Life!

st
 Confess your wrong.  
• 1John  1:5-10    Now  the  message  that  we  have  heard  from  his  Son  
and announce is this: God is light, and there is no darkness at all 
in him. If, then, we say that we have fellowship with him, yet at the 
same time  live  in  the  darkness,  we  are  lying  both  in  our  words  
and in our actions. But if we live in the light---just as he is in the 
light---then we have fellowship with one another, and the blood of 
Jesus, his Son, purifies us from every sin. If we say that we have 
no  sin,  we  deceive  ourselves,  and  there  is  no  truth  in  us.   But  if  
we confess our sins to God, he will keep his promise and do what 
is  right:  he  will  forgive  us  our  sins  and  purify  us  from  all  our  
wrongdoing.    If  we  say  that  we  have  not  sinned,  we  make  a  liar  
out of God, and his word is not in us.  
Admit  your  sins  to  God  right  now  for  ALL  your  sins—including  
fortune telling.  Admit  
These sins are wrong... and you are wrong for doing them. 
 
Prayer of confession (the Sinner’s Prayer): 
Father  God,  I  confess  that  I  have  been  sinning  by  fortune  telling,  
and  I  also  have  many  more  sins  in  my  life.    I  confess  that  I  am  I  
sinner, and I need to be saved.  Please forgive me of my sins.  Save 
me  from  my  sins.    Save  me  form  the  fires  of  Hell.    I  believe  you  
sent  your  Son  Jesus  Christ  into  this  world  to  die  for  my  sins.    I  
repent from my sins today, and I will obey your word.  I proclaim 
Jesus Christ to be the Lord of my life, and I ask him to remove the 
curses that are upon me and my family.  Give me the strength to be

same time  live  in  the  darkness,  we  are  lying  both  in  our  words  
and in our actions. But if we live in the light---just as he is in the 
light---then we have fellowship with one another, and the blood of 
Jesus, his Son, purifies us from every sin. If we say that we have 
no  sin,  we  deceive  ourselves,  and  there  is  no  truth  in  us.   But  if  
we confess our sins to God, he will keep his promise and do what 
is  right:  he  will  forgive  us  our  sins  and  purify  us  from  all  our  
wrongdoing.    If  we  say  that  we  have  not  sinned,  we  make  a  liar  
out of God, and his word is not in us.  
Admit  your  sins  to  God  right  now  for  ALL  your  sins—including  
fortune telling.  Admit  
These sins are wrong... and you are wrong for doing them. 
 
Prayer of confession (the Sinner’s Prayer): 
Father  God,  I  confess  that  I  have  been  sinning  by  fortune  telling,  
and  I  also  have  many  more  sins  in  my  life.    I  confess  that  I  am  I  
sinner, and I need to be saved.  Please forgive me of my sins.  Save 
me  from  my  sins.    Save  me  form  the  fires  of  Hell.    I  believe  you  
sent  your  Son  Jesus  Christ  into  this  world  to  die  for  my  sins.    I  
repent from my sins today, and I will obey your word.  I proclaim 
Jesus Christ to be the Lord of my life, and I ask him to remove the 
curses that are upon me and my family.  Give me the strength to be 
75 
 
1
st
 Confess your wrong.  
• 1John  1:5-10    Now  the  message  that  we  have  heard  from  his  Son  
and announce is this: God is light, and there is no darkness at all 
in him. If, then, we say that we have fellowship with him, yet at the

Prayer of confession (the Sinner’s Prayer): 
Father  God,  I  confess  that  I  have  been  sinning  by  fortune  telling,  
and  I  also  have  many  more  sins  in  my  life.    I  confess  that  I  am  I  
sinner, and I need to be saved.  Please forgive me of my sins.  Save 
me  from  my  sins.    Save  me  form  the  fires  of  Hell.    I  believe  you  
sent  your  Son  Jesus  Christ  into  this  world  to  die  for  my  sins.    I  
repent from my sins today, and I will obey your word.  I proclaim 
Jesus Christ to be the Lord of my life, and I ask him to remove the 
curses that are upon me and my family.  Give me the strength to be 

76 
 
obedient to your word. I pray this prayer in your Son’s name, Jesus 
Christ of Nazareth, the Living Son of God.  Amen. 
 
Just  because  you  confessed  Him,  it  does  not  mean  you  have  a  
one  way  ticket  to  Heaven.    However,  it  does  mean  you  have  
taken the first step in turning your life over to Jesus.  Now you 
must truly repent, and walk away from your sins. 
• Matthew 7:21-23  Not everyone who calls me their Lord will get 
into  the  kingdom  of  heaven.  Only  the  ones  who  obey  my  Father  
in  heaven  will  get  in.  On  the  day  of  judgment  many  will  call  me  
their Lord. They will say, "We preached in your name, and in your 
name  we  forced  out  demons  and  worked  many  miracles."    But  I  
will  tell  them,  "I  will  have  nothing  to  do  with  you!  Get  out  of  my  
sight, you evil people!"  
Obey God and stay away from the things that are evil.  Stay in Jesus.  
• 1John  3:6    So  everyone  who  lives  in  union  with  Christ  does  not  
continue to sin; but whoever continues to sin has never seen him 
or known him.

gossip,  judging  and  lying.  After  I  read  the  Bible  completely  
through,  I  stopped  reading  the  Bible  and  started  reading  other

 Read the Word of God. 
When I started reading the Word of God every day is when I was 
delivered of the sins that were in my life.  I read the entire Bible in 
a  year,  and  in  that  year  I  was  delivered  from;  drinking,  cursing,

gossip,  judging  and  lying.  After  I  read  the  Bible  completely  
through,  I  stopped  reading  the  Bible  and  started  reading  other  

77 
 
Christian  books  instead.    About  two  weeks  went  by  and  I  found  
myself judging, getting angry, and gossiping again.  God revealed 
to  me  that  these  things  slipped  back  into  my  life  because  I  
neglected his word.  Since that day, I have read the Bible every 
day before I even leave my home.  Listen to what the Bible says 
about how important the Word is; 
• Joshua 1:7-8  Just be determined, be confident; and make sure that 
you obey the whole Law
 that my servant Moses gave you. Do not 
neglect  any  part  of  it  and  you  will  succeed  wherever  you  go.    Be  
sure  that  the  book  of  the  Law  is  always  read  in  your  worship.  
Study  it  day  and  night,
  and  make  sure  that  you  obey  everything  
written in it. Then you will be prosperous and successful.  
• John  8:31-32    Then  said  Jesus  to  those  Jews  which  believed  on  
him, If ye continue in my word, then are ye my disciples indeed; 
And ye shall know the truth, and the truth shall Set you free.  
Do  you  desire  to  be  successful  and  prosperous  in  every  way;  
spiritual, physical, and financial?  The Word of God shows us the 
key,  and  they  are  Obedience.    The  Bible  tells  us  Deuteronomy  28  
that  if  we  Obey  god  and  his  law,  we  will  receive  prosperity  and  
success.  Furthermore, we will be SET FREE from all the sin that 
is in our lives, all the additions, and even all the desires.  
• Hebrews 4:12  The word of God is alive and active,
 sharper than 
any double-edged sword. It cuts all the way through, to where soul 
and spirit meet, to where joints and marrow come together. It jude 
the desires and thoughts of the heart.

78 
 
When you apply the word of God to your life, the Holy Spirit will 
set  you  free,  but  first  we  need  to  plant  the  seed  that  produces  the  
fruit of the Holy Spirit to set us free.  The Word of God is seed that 
produces  Fruit;  the  fruit  of  the  Holy  Spirit.    If  we  don’t  plant  the  
seed and  apply  the  word  to  our  lives,
  we  will  not  inherit  the  
Kingdom of Heaven 
• 1Peter 1:23  having been Born again, not of corruptible seed, but 
of incorruptible seed, through the word of God, which liveth and 
abideth 
• John 3:3  Jesus answered, "I am telling you the truth: no one can 
see the Kingdom of God without being born again."  
Buy  you  a  Bible,  and  get  one  you  can  understand.    Do  not  ask  
someone to buy it for you, but you make the investment.  Go to a 
Bible   book   store,   look   around   and   see   which   one   you   can   
understand.    If  you  cannot  read,  I  strongly  suggest  you  learn,  but  
until then get the Bible on CD. The first Bible I ever read through 
was a New Living Translation.   
After  you  get  a  Bible  start  a  Daily  Devotion.    What  I  do  every  
morning before I do anything is; I get up, wash up and Read up.  I 
read 2 Chapters a day, one Chapter in the Old Testament and one 
in  the  New  Testament.    I  would  advise  you  to  start  Reading  the  
New  Testament  first.    Begin  reading  2  Chapters  a  day,  and  your  
life  will  never  be  the  same!    HOWEVER  YOU  DECIDE  TO  
READ, JUST START READING AND APPLYING!

RD
 Attend Church   

79 
 
• Exodus  20:8-11    "Observe  the  Sabbath  and  keep  it  holy.      You  
have  six  days  in  which  to  do  your  work,  but  the  seventh  day  is  a  
day of rest dedicated to me. On that day no one is to work---neither 
you,  your  children,  your  slaves,  your  animals,  nor  the  foreigners  
who live in your country.  In six days I, the LORD, made the earth, 
the sky, the seas, and everything in them, but on the seventh day I 
rested. That is why I, the LORD, blessed the Sabbath and made it 
holy. 
• Hebrews  10:25    Some  people  have  gotten  out  of  the  habit  of  
meeting for worship, but we must not do that. We should keep on 
encouraging each other, especially since you know that the day of 
the Lord's coming is getting closer.  
You  need  to  attend  a  church.    Even  Jesus  went  to  the  synagogue 
every  Sabbath  to  hear  the  word  and  worship  God.    Church  is  
important  because  it  is  where  the  Word  of  God  is  preached  and  
explained.  The  Bible  tells  us  “Faith  comes  by  HEARING  and  
Hearing  by  the  Word  of  God.”    We  go  to  church  because  God  
wants to encourage us by His words.  There have been many times 
I  went  to  church  completely  burdened  and  distracted,  and  the  
Pastor would give me a Word from God.  It was like God was only 
talking  to  me.    I  received  the  Word  I  needed  to  get  through  my  
situation.  That’s what church does for us.  Bottom line; you cannot 
live a life that pleases God if you do not go to Church. 
• Hebrews 11:6  But without faith it is impossible to please him: for 
he  that  cometh  to  God  must  believe  that  he  is,  and  that he is a 
rewarded of them that diligently seek him.  
79 
 
• Exodus  20:8-11    "Observe  the  Sabbath  and  keep  it  holy.      You  
have  six  days  in  which  to  do  your  work,  but  the  seventh  day  is  a  
day of rest dedicated to me. On that day no one is to work---neither 
you,  your  children,  your  slaves,  your  animals,  nor  the  foreigners  
who live in your country.  In six days I, the LORD, made the earth, 
the sky, the seas, and everything in them, but on the seventh day I 
rested. That is why I, the LORD, blessed the Sabbath and made it 
holy. 
• Hebrews  10:25    Some  people  have  gotten  out  of  the  habit  of  
meeting for worship, but we must not do that. We should keep on 
encouraging each other, especially since you know that the day of 
the Lord's coming is getting closer.  
You  need  to  attend  a  church.    Even  Jesus  went  to  the  synagogue 
every  Sabbath  to  hear  the  word  and  worship  God.    Church  is  
important  because  it  is  where  the  Word  of  God  is  preached  and  
explained.  The  Bible  tells  us  “Faith  comes  by  HEARING  and  
Hearing  by  the  Word  of  God.”    We  go  to  church  because  God  
wants to encourage us by His words.  There have been many times 
I  went  to  church  completely  burdened  and  distracted,  and  the  
Pastor would give me a Word from God.  It was like God was only 
talking  to  me.    I  received  the  Word  I  needed  to  get  through  my  
situation.  That’s what church does for us.  Bottom line; you cannot 
live a life that pleases God if you do not go to Church. 
• Hebrews 11:6  But without faith it is impossible to please him: for 
he  that  cometh  to  God  must  believe  that  he  is,  and  that he is a 
rewarded of them that diligently seek him.  
79

80 
 
The  Greek  Definition  for  the  Word  diligently  in  the  Bible  is:  
ekzeteo 
Thayer Definition: 
1) to seek out, search for 
2) to seek out, i.e. investigate, scrutinize 
3) to seek out for one’s self, beg, craveble 
4) to requireble 
This is what church does for us; it investigates the Bible, searches 
out the Scriptures, and explains them so we can apply them to our 
lives.    We  also  have  to  diligently  seek  God  ourselves  as  well;  
through Prayer, Worship and the Word, and church helps teach us 
how to do this corporately and privately.   
 
Another important thing church provides is a group of people you 
can  fellowship  with,  people  who  can  encourage  you,  people  who  
will help you, and make you stronger. 
• Acts  2:41-44    Many  of  them  believed  his  message  and  were  
baptized,  and  about  three  thousand  people  were  added  to  the  
group that day. They spent their time in learning from the apostles, 
taking part in the fellowship, and sharing in the fellowship meals
 
and  the  prayers.  Many  miracles  and  wonders  were  being  done  
through  the  apostles,  and  everyone  was  filled  with  awe.    All  the  
believers  continued  together  in  close  fellowship  and  shared  their  
belongings with one another.

• Romans  1:11-12    For  I  want  very  much  to  see  you,  in  order  to  
share  a  spiritual  blessing  with  you  to  make  you  strong.  What  I  
mean is that both you and I will be helped at the same time, you by 
my faith and I by yours.  
Christian Fellowship is key; people to encourage you, to pray for you, 
and to help you in 
your Christian walk.  
 
What kind of Church? 
I  prefer  the  following  denominations;  Church  of  God  or  the  
Assemblies  of  God.    The  reason  why  is  because  they  base  their  
doctrine on The Whole Word of God.   I am a licensed Church of 
God Pastor, so I strongly recommend one of these churches.  Also, 
the Church of God is a worldwide denomination; we have churches 
in over 160 countries. You can go on the internet and find church 
nearest  you  at  www.churchofGod.org
  or  any  assemblies  of  God  
teaching church.

82 
 
Chapter 15 
Gods Great Plan 
When  you  repent  and  surrender  your  life  to  God,  he  has  an  
awesome plan for your life. Your culture might tell you since you 
are a woman you cannot do anything for God or be used by God, 
but that just is not true.  There was a woman who lived in the time 
of  Jesus  named  Mary.  The  bible  says  that  Mary  was  a  “known”  
sinner,  meaning  she  was  famous  for  her  sin.    Mary  had  an  
experience  with  Jesus;  he  forgave  her  when  everyone  else  was  
condemning  her.    The  power  of  forgiveness  and  the  Love  Jesus  
showed Mary captured her so much, she followed Jesus throughout 
all His Ministry.   
When  Jesus  Resurrected  from  the  dead,  you  would  think  the  first  
person who would see Him would have been His Disciples or even 
His Mother, but it was not any of them.  The first person to see the 
Resurrected Jesus Christ was the “known” sinner, Mary. 
• Mark  16:9    [After  Jesus  rose  from  death  early  on  Sunday,  he  
appeared  first  to  Mary  Magdalene,  from  whom  he  had  driven  out  
seven demons. 
If  you  follow  Jesus  (the  Word  of  God)  as  Mary  followed  Jesus,  
you  will  also  see  Resurrection.    You  will  see  resurrection  in  your  
Marriage,  your  Children,  your  Family,  your  finances,  and  in  your  
relationship with Jesus Christ the living Son of God. 
 
God has a great Plan for your life.  
82

83 
 
• Jeremiah 29:11-13  I alone know the plans I have for you, plans to 
bring  you  prosperity  and  not  disaster,  plans  to  bring  about  the  
future you hope for.  Then you will call to me. You will come and 
pray to me, and I will answer you.  You will seek me, and you will 
find me because you will seek me with all your heart.  
 You might think you are the worse sinner.  Mary probably thought 
the  same  thing  after  seven  demons  left  her,  but  read  what  Jesus  
said... 
• Matthew  9:12-13    Jesus  heard  them  and  answered,  "People  who  
are well do not need a doctor, but only those who are sick. Go and 
find  out  what  is  meant  by  the  scripture  that  says:  'It  is  kindness  
that  I  want,  not  animal  sacrifices.'  I  have  not  come  to  call  
respectable people, but outcasts."  
Jesus is calling you, just like he called Mary.  Jesus will use you, just 
like he used Mary. 
• 1Colossians  1:26-29    Now  remember  what  you  were,  my  friends,  
when  God  called  you.  From  the  human  point  of  view  few  of  you  
were  wise  or  powerful  or  of  high  social  standing.  God  purposely  
chose  what  the  world  considers  nonsense  in  order  to  shame  the  
wise,  and  he  chose  what  the  world  considers  weak  in  order  to  
shame the powerful. He chose what the world looks down on and 
despises and thinks is nothing, in order to destroy what the world 
thinks  is  important.  This  means  that  no  one  can  boast  in  God's  
presence.

thinks  is  important.  This  means  that  no  one  can  boast  in  God's  
presence.  
 
83 
 
• Jeremiah 29:11-13  I alone know the plans I have for you, plans to 
bring  you  prosperity  and  not  disaster,  plans  to  bring  about  the  
future you hope for.  Then you will call to me. You will come and 
pray to me, and I will answer you.  You will seek me, and you will 
find me because you will seek me with all your heart.  
 You might think you are the worse sinner.  Mary probably thought 
the  same  thing  after  seven  demons  left  her,  but  read  what  Jesus  
said... 
• Matthew  9:12-13    Jesus  heard  them  and  answered,  "People  who  
are well do not need a doctor, but only those who are sick. Go and 
find  out  what  is  meant  by  the  scripture  that  says:  'It  is  kindness  
that  I  want,  not  animal  sacrifices.'  I  have  not  come  to  call  
respectable people, but outcasts."  
Jesus is calling you, just like he called Mary.  Jesus will use you, just 
like he used Mary. 
• 1Colossians  1:26-29    Now  remember  what  you  were,  my  friends,  
when  God  called  you.  From  the  human  point  of  view  few  of  you  
were  wise  or  powerful  or  of  high  social  standing.  God  purposely  
chose  what  the  world  considers  nonsense  in  order  to  shame  the  
wise,  and  he  chose  what  the  world  considers  weak  in  order  to  
shame the powerful. He chose what the world looks down on and 
despises and thinks is nothing, in order to destroy what the world 
thinks  is  important.  This  means  that  no  one  can  boast  in  God's  
presence.  
 

84 
 
But  remember...  Matthew  22:14    For  many  are  called,  but  few  
chosen.  The ones who are chosen to receive Gods Awesome Plan 
are the ones who accept and Follow the Plan. 
• Matthew  7:24-27    "So  then,  anyone  who  hears  these  words  of  
mine  and  obeys  them  is  like  a  wise  man  who  built  his  house  on  
rock. The rain poured down, the rivers flooded over, and the wind 
blew  hard  against  that  house.  But  it  did  not  fall,  because  it  was  
built  on  rock.  "But  anyone  who  hears  these  words  of  mine  and  
does  not  obey  them  is  like  a  foolish  man  who  built  his  house  on  
sand.    The  rain  poured  down,  the  rivers  flooded  over,  the  wind  
blew  hard  against  that  house,  and  it  fell.  And  what  a  terrible  fall  
that was!"  
Let  us  start  following  God’s  word...  not  our  own  opinions  and  
other people’s advice...but what the Word of God says.   
• Proverbs 3:5-8  Trust in the LORD with all your heart. Never rely 
on  what  you  think  you  know.    Remember  the  LORD  in  everything  
you  do,  and  he  will  show  you  the  right  way.    Never  let  yourself  
think that you are wiser than you are; simply obey the LORD and 
refuse to do wrong. If you do, it will be like good medicine, healing 
your wounds and easing your pains.  
 
God  has  a  Plan,  and  he  has  written  it  down  for  you.    Follow  his  
plan, and you will receive all the benefits it has to offer.

Conclusion 
 
Don’t  forget  what  you  have  been  taught  in  this  Book!!!  This 
book was not meant to be for the purpose of entertainment, but for 
teaching and instruction. 
• Proverbs  3:1-14    My  child,  don't  forget  what  I  teach  you.  Always  
remember what I tell you to do. If you are obedient to my teaching, 
I  will  give  you  a  long  and  prosperous  life.  Never  let  go  of  loyalty  
and  faithfulness.  Tie  them  around  your  neck;  write  them  on  your  
heart. If you do this, both God and people will be pleased with you. 
Trust  in  the  LORD  with  all  your  heart.  Never  rely  on  what  you  
think you know. Remember the LORD in everything you do, and he 
will  show  you  the  right  way.  Never  let  yourself  think  that  you  are  
wiser than you are; simply obey the LORD and refuse to do wrong. 
If  you  do,  it  will  be  like  good  medicine,  healing  your  wounds  and  
easing  your  pains.  Honor  the  LORD  by  making  him  an  offering  
from the best of all that your land produces. If you do, your barns 
will be filled with grain, and you will have too much wine to store 
it  all.  My  child,  when  the  LORD  corrects  you,  pay  close  attention  
and  take  it  as  a  warning.  The  LORD  corrects  those  he  loves,  as  
parents correct a child of whom they are proud. Happy is anyone 
who  becomes  wise---who  comes  to  have  understanding.  There  is  
more  profit  in  it  than  there  is  in  silver;  it  is  worth  more  to  you  
than gold.  
 
85

86 
 
I  have  known  many  people  throughout  my  life  who  have  tried  to  
justify the sin of fortune telling, but God says differently... as you 
have  discovered  in  this  book.    If  you  apply  what  you  have  read,  
you will live a blessed life.  If you do not apply it, you will live a 
cursed life.  If you apply what was written, you will go to Heaven.  
If you do not apply them, you will go to hell. 
 
Please understand that I did not write this book to be popular with 
people, but I wrote this book to be Obedient to God.  If you have 
complaints  or  if  you  are  mad  about  the  Truth  that  has  been  
revealed in this book, it is not against me but against God.   
 
Jesus said... 
• Luke 9:26  If you are ashamed of me and of my teaching, then the 
Son of Man will be ashamed of you when he comes in his glory and 
in the glory of the Father and of the holy angels.

Joshua  24:15  “As  for  me  and  my  house,  we  choose  to  serve  the  
Lord”  
Joshua made a choice for him in his house they chose to serve the 
Lord he told the rest of the people they can do what ever they 
wanted to do but for him and his house he chose right. So you 
have that same choice. What do you choose?
